  "use strict";

  const PROTOCOL_VERSION = "mega-r48";
  const now = () => Date.now();

  const stableText = value => {
    const seen = new WeakSet();
    const normalize = input => {
      if (!input || typeof input !== "object") return input;
      if (seen.has(input)) return "[Circular]";
      seen.add(input);
      if (Array.isArray(input)) return input.map(normalize);
      const out = {};
      for (const key of Object.keys(input).sort()) {
        if (key === "checksum" || key === "createdAt") continue;
        out[key] = normalize(input[key]);
      }
      return out;
    };
    return JSON.stringify(normalize(value));
  };
  const hashText = text => {
    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) { h ^= text.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619); }
    return (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
  };
  const defineDerived = (object, key, value) => {
    try { Object.defineProperty(object, key, { value, writable:true, configurable:true, enumerable:false }); }
    catch { object[key] = value; }
    return value;
  };
  const currentGame = () => { try { return typeof game !== "undefined" ? game : window.game; } catch { return window.game; } };

  const GameConfig = Object.freeze({
    protocolVersion: PROTOCOL_VERSION,
    maxEnergyDefault: 4,
    maxEventLog: 1200,
    maxOnlineEventTail: 128,
    maxOnlineLogTail: 160,
    animationFallbackMs: 12000,
    networkSyncTimeoutMs: 12000,
    phases: Object.freeze([
      "IDLE","BATTLE_STARTING","TURN_START","START_OF_TURN_EFFECTS","WAITING_FOR_DEPLOYMENT",
      "WAITING_FOR_ENERGY_GAIN","WAITING_FOR_ENERGY_REPLACEMENT","WAITING_FOR_ACTION","WAITING_FOR_TARGET",
      "WAITING_FOR_PAYMENT","EXECUTING_ACTION","WAITING_FOR_ANIMATION","RESOLVING_EFFECTS","CHECKING_DEATHS",
      "CHECKING_VICTORY","TURN_END","SWITCHING_TURN","SYNCING","BATTLE_ENDED"
    ])
  });

  const IdFactory = (() => {
    let eventSerial = 0, transactionSerial = 0, energySerial = 0, lockSerial = 0;
    const token = (prefix, battleId="global") => `${prefix}:${battleId}:${now().toString(36)}:${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    return {
      battle(seed="battle") { return token("battle", String(seed)); },
      event(battleId, sequence) { return `event:${battleId}:${sequence || ++eventSerial}`; },
      transaction(battleId) { return `tx:${battleId}:${++transactionSerial}`; },
      energy(entityId="entity") { return `energy:${entityId}:${++energySerial}`; },
      lock(reason) { return `lock:${reason}:${++lockSerial}`; },
      slot(team, zone, index) { return `slot:${team}:${zone}:${index}`; }
    };
  })();

  const EventBus = (() => {
    const listeners = new Map();
    const on = (type, listener) => {
      if (typeof listener !== "function") throw new TypeError("listener must be a function");
      const set = listeners.get(type) || new Set(); set.add(listener); listeners.set(type, set);
      return () => set.delete(listener);
    };
    const emit = event => {
      for (const type of [event.type, "*"]) {
        for (const listener of [...(listeners.get(type) || [])]) {
          try { listener(event); }
          catch (error) { console.error(`[R48 EventBus:${type}]`, error); queueMicrotask(() => { throw error; }); }
        }
      }
      return event;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ on, emit, clear(type) { type ? listeners.delete(type) : listeners.clear(); }, listenerCount(type) { return (listeners.get(type) || new Set()).size; } });
  })();

  const TimerManager = window.TimerManager;


  const InputLockManager = (() => {
    const locks = new Map();
    let clockToken = null;
    const roots = () => [document.querySelector(".app"), document.getElementById("modal")].filter(Boolean);
    const applyDomLock = on => {
      document.body.classList.toggle("time-input-locked",!!on);
      for (const root of roots()) {
        try { root.inert = !!on; } catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error,"InputLockManager.applyDomLock"); }
        root.setAttribute("aria-hidden", on ? "true" : "false");
      }
    };
    const updateDataset = () => {
      if (locks.size) document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked = "true";
      else delete document.documentElement.dataset.inputLocked;
    };
    const lock = (reason, owner="runtime", options={}) => {
      const normalized = String(reason || "unknown");
      const token = IdFactory.lock(normalized);
      const runtime = window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.() || {};
      const createdAt = now();
      locks.set(token, {
        token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt,
        battleId:options.battleId ?? currentGame()?.battleId ?? null,
        sessionGeneration:Number(options.sessionGeneration ?? runtime.generation ?? 0),
        revision:Number(options.revision ?? currentGame()?.networkRevision ?? 0),
        timeoutAt:Number(options.timeoutMs) > 0 ? createdAt + Number(options.timeoutMs) : null,
        cleanupPath:String(options.cleanupPath || "owner-finally-or-battle-cleanup"),
      });
      updateDataset();
      EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_LOCKED", token, reason:normalized, owner, createdAt:now() });
      return token;
    };
    const unlock = tokenOrReason => {
      let count = 0;
      if (locks.has(tokenOrReason)) {
        if (tokenOrReason === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(tokenOrReason); count=1;
      } else {
        for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === tokenOrReason) {
          if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
          locks.delete(token); count++;
        }
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (count) EventBus.emit({ type:"INPUT_UNLOCKED", tokenOrReason, count, createdAt:now() });
      return count;
    };
    const releaseClockOwner = () => {
      if (clockToken && locks.has(clockToken)) locks.delete(clockToken);
      clockToken = null;
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.reason === "clock-animation" && entry.owner === "timeSetInputLock") locks.delete(token);
      updateDataset();
    };
    const setClockLock = (on, options={}) => {
      const holdBusy = options.holdBusy !== false;
      if (on) {
        if (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken)) clockToken = lock("clock-animation",options.owner || "timeSetInputLock");
        timeInputLocked = true;
        applyDomLock(true);
        if (holdBusy && typeof busy !== "undefined") {
          if (!busy) timeInputLockBusyOwned = true;
          busy = true;
        }
      } else {
        timeInputLocked = false;
        applyDomLock(false);
        releaseClockOwner();
        if (timeInputLockBusyOwned) {
          timeInputLockBusyOwned = false;
          if (!(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand)) busy = false;
        }
      }
      return timeInputLocked;
    };
    const clockVisualPresent = () => !!document.querySelector(".time-clock-action-overlay,.time-hyper-clock,.time-recall-fx,.time-clock-cinematic,.time-skill-fx");
    const reconcileLegacyClock = (reason="reconcile") => {
      const declared = !!timeInputLocked || document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked");
      const legitimate = !!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance || clockVisualPresent();
      if (declared && !legitimate) {
        setClockLock(false,{holdBusy:false,owner:`reconcile:${reason}`});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-stale-clock"};
      }
      if (declared && legitimate && (!clockToken || !locks.has(clockToken))) {
        setClockLock(true,{holdBusy:false,owner:"timeSetInputLock"});
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"adopted-clock"};
      }
      if (!declared && clockToken) {
        releaseClockOwner();
        return {changed:true,reason,state:"released-orphan-token"};
      }
      applyDomLock(declared);
      return {changed:false,reason,state:declared?"locked":"unlocked"};
    };
    const releaseBattle = battleId => {
      for (const [token, entry] of [...locks]) if (entry.battleId === battleId) {
        if (token === clockToken) clockToken = null;
        locks.delete(token);
      }
      updateDataset();
      if (!clockToken && timeInputLocked && currentGame()?.battleId === battleId) {
        timeInputLocked=false; applyDomLock(false); timeInputLockBusyOwned=false;
      }
    };
    const reasons = () => [...new Set([...locks.values()].map(x=>x.reason))];
    const snapshot = () => [...locks.values()].map(x=>({...x}));
    const clockState = () => ({token:clockToken,locked:!!timeInputLocked||document.body.classList.contains("time-input-locked"),visual:clockVisualPresent(),pending:!!currentGame()?.pendingClockTurnAdvance});
    LocalInputEligibility.bindProviders({
      locks:snapshot,
      clock:clockState,
      online:g=>{const runtime=window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.()||{};return{
        role:p2p?.role || null,
        connected:!!p2p?.connected && !!p2p?.conn?.open,
        battleStarted:(!!p2p?.battleStarted || !!g?.battleStarted) && !(p2p?.role==="host" && (p2p?.onlineStartPending || p2p?.startCommitPacket) && !p2p?.startCommitAck) && !(p2p?.role==="guest" && p2p?.guestStartAwaitingHostCommit),
        reconnecting:!!p2p?.reconnecting || !!p2p?.connectionInterrupted || runtime.state === "RECONNECTING" || (!!runtime.generation&&runtime.committed&&!runtime.routesReady),
        pendingActionId:(typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" ? onlinePendingCommand?.packet?.actionId : null) || p2p?.pendingActionId || null,
        authoritativeRevision:Number(p2p?.lastAuthoritativeRevision || (typeof onlineAuthoritativeRevision !== "undefined" ? onlineAuthoritativeRevision : 0) || 0),
        busy:!!busy,

    if (typeof RNG === "function") { const rng = new RNG(data.seed); rng.s = snap.rngState || data.rng?.s || rng.s; data.rng = rng; }
    for (const key of Object.keys(g)) delete g[key];
    for (const key of ["entities","slots","appliedEventIds"]) {
      try { delete g[key]; } catch {}
    }
    Object.assign(g,data);
    if (snap.token != null) g.token = snap.token;
    StateFactory.decorate(g);
    defineDerived(g,"appliedEventIds",new Set((g.eventLog || []).map(event => event?.eventId).filter(Boolean)));
    return g;
  };

  const ACTION_FAULT_PHASES = Object.freeze([
    "validation-after","draft-after","payment-before","payment-after","energy-proposal-before","energy-proposal-after","rng-before","rng-after","damage-before","first-hit-after","next-hit-before","damage-after","healing-before","healing-after","status-before","status-after","schedule-before","schedule-after","death-before","death-after","replacement-pending-after","victory-before","victory-after","terminal-pending-after","terminal-commit-before","event-publication-before","remote-ack-before","render-before","vfx-before","audio-before"
  ]);
  const ActionFaultInjector = (()=>{
    let armed=null, hits=[];
    return Object.freeze({
      phases:()=>[...ACTION_FAULT_PHASES],
      arm:phase=>{if(!ACTION_FAULT_PHASES.includes(phase))throw new Error(`unknown fault phase ${phase}`);armed=phase;hits=[];},
      clear:()=>{armed=null;hits=[];},
      hit(phase){hits.push(phase);if(armed===phase){armed=null;const error=new Error(`CP32_FAULT:${phase}`);error.code="CP32_FAULT_INJECTED";error.phase=phase;throw error;}},
      debug:()=>({armed,hits:[...hits]}),
    });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"ActionFaultInjector",{value:ActionFaultInjector,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const PresentationFailureChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ presentationSequence:events.length+1, timestamp:Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if(events.length>512)events.splice(0,events.length-512);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x=>({...x}));
    const clear = () => { events.length=0; };
    return Object.freeze({emit,snapshot,clear});
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"PresentationFailureChannel",{value:PresentationFailureChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if (events.length > 2048) events.splice(0, events.length - 2048);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const forAction = actionId => events.filter(x => x.actionId === actionId).map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"TransactionAuditChannel",{value:TransactionAuditChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});
  const ActionTransactionManager = (() => {
    const committed = new Map(), failed = new Map();
    const begin = (g,t,s,action) => {
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      if (seen.has(actionId) || rejected.has(actionId)) return { duplicate:true, actionId, terminalStatus:seen.has(actionId)?"committed":"failed" };
      const transaction = { transactionId:IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId), actionId, teamId:t, slot:s, action, startedAt:now(), eventSequenceAtStart:Number(g.eventSequence||0), snapshot:snapshotState(g) };
      g.pendingTransaction = transaction; g.pendingAction = action; g.phase = "EXECUTING_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{transactionId:transaction.transactionId,actionId,actionType:action?.type}});
      return transaction;
    };
    const commit = (g,tx) => {
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); seen.add(tx.actionId); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_COMMITTED",{teamId:tx.teamId,payload:{transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,durationMs:now()-tx.startedAt}});
    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const diffUnits = (before, g, causedByEventId=null, eventSequenceAtStart=0) => {
    EntityRegistry.sync(g);
    const previous = new Map();
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(before.raw);
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const zone of ["field","bench","graveyard","summons"]) for (const unit of raw.teams?.[team]?.[zone] || []) if (unit) previous.set(unit.entityId || unit.uid,unit);
    } catch (error) { console.error("[R48] diff snapshot parse failed",error); }
    for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities || {})) {
      const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid, old = previous.get(id); if (!old) continue;
      const common = { recipientEntityId:id, teamId:typeof teamOf === "function" ? teamOf(g,unit) : null, causedByEventId };
      const hasDetailedDamage = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ev.type === "DAMAGE_APPLIED" &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&

        ["HEAL_APPLIED","HEALING_APPLIED"].includes(ev.type) &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&

      );

          damageType:"stateDiff", before:{hp:old.hp,shield:old.shield}, after:{hp:unit.hp,shield:unit.shield},
          amount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp), actionId:g?.pendingTransaction?.actionId || null,
        });
        else if(g?.pendingTransaction&&g.phase==="EXECUTING_ACTION") GenericBattleStatisticsOwner.recordHealing(g,{source:null,target:unit,requestedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),actualAppliedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),reason:"action-state-diff",effectId:"actionStateDiff",actionId:g.pendingTransaction.actionId||null,transactionId:g.pendingTransaction.transactionId||null});
      }
      if (Number(unit.shield)!==Number(old.shield)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SHIELD_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.shield,after:unit.shield}});
      const oldStatus=stableText(old.statuses||{}),newStatus=stableText(unit.statuses||{});
      if(oldStatus!==newStatus) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"STATUS_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.statuses||{},after:unit.statuses||{}}});
      const oldEnergy=(old.energy||[]).map(e=>({energyId:e.energyId,c:e.c,a:e.a})),newEnergy=EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit);
      if(stableText(oldEnergy)!==stableText(newEnergy)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENERGY_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:oldEnergy,after:newEnergy}});
      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
    reconcile(g) {

      setTimeout(fn,delay,label="session-timeout",options={}){const phase=options.phase||"SESSION",capture=this.capture(label,phase);let id=0;id=window.TimerManager.setTimeout(()=>{this.resources.delete(id);this.guard(capture,fn)();},Math.max(0,Number(delay)||0));this.resources.set(id,{kind:"timeout",phase,label,cancel:()=>window.TimerManager.clear(id)});return id;}
      setInterval(fn,delay,label="session-interval",options={}){const phase=options.phase||"SESSION",capture=this.capture(label,phase);const id=window.TimerManager.setInterval(this.guard(capture,fn),Math.max(16,Number(delay)||0));this.resources.set(id,{kind:"interval",phase,label,cancel:()=>window.TimerManager.clear(id)});return id;}
      requestAnimationFrame(fn,label="session-raf",options={}){const phase=options.phase||this.phase,capture=this.capture(label,phase);const id=window.TimerManager.requestAnimationFrame(()=>{this.resources.delete(id);this.guard(capture,fn)();});this.resources.set(id,{kind:"raf",phase,label,cancel:()=>window.TimerManager.clear(id)});return id;}
      listen(target,event,fn,options,label=`listener:${event}`){if(!target?.addEventListener)return()=>false;const capture=this.capture(label,"SESSION"),guarded=this.guard(capture,fn);target.addEventListener(event,guarded,options);const key={};this.resources.set(key,{kind:"listener",phase:"SESSION",label,cancel:()=>target.removeEventListener(event,guarded,options)});return()=>this.cancelResource(key);}
      track(promise,label="session-promise",options={}){const phase=options.phase||this.phase,capture=this.capture(label,phase),record={label,phase,capture,cancel:typeof options.cancel==="function"?options.cancel:null};this.promises.add(record);return Promise.resolve(promise).then(value=>this.valid(capture)?value:undefined,error=>{if(this.valid(capture))throw error;return undefined;}).finally(()=>this.promises.delete(record));}
      transaction(kind,data={}){const id=`${kind}:${this.generation}:${Date.now()}:${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`,row={id,kind,state:"OPEN",createdAt:Date.now(),...data};this.transactions.set(kind,row);this.record("transaction-open",{kind,id});return row;}
      completeTransaction(kind,detail={}){const row=this.transactions.get(kind);if(!row)return false;Object.assign(row,{state:"COMPLETED",completedAt:Date.now(),...detail});this.transactions.delete(kind);this.record("transaction-complete",{kind,id:row.id});return true;}
      cancelResource(key){const row=this.resources.get(key);if(!row)return false;try{row.cancel?.();}catch{}this.resources.delete(key);return true;}
      cancelPhase(phase,reason="phase-transition"){let count=0;for(const [key,row] of [...this.resources])if(row.phase===phase){this.cancelResource(key);count++;}for(const row of [...this.promises])if(row.phase===phase){try{row.cancel?.(reason);}catch{}this.promises.delete(row);count++;}this.record("phase-resources-cancelled",{phase,reason,count});return count;}
      transitionPhase(next,reason="phase-transition"){next=String(next||"ENTRY");const prior=this.phase;if(prior===next)return this.projectionEpoch;this.cancelPhase(prior,reason);this.phase=next;this.projectionEpoch++;this.record("phase-transition",{from:prior,to:next,reason});return this.projectionEpoch;}
      close(reason="scope-close"){if(this.closed)return false;this.closed=true;for(const key of [...this.resources.keys()])this.cancelResource(key);for(const row of [...this.promises]){try{row.cancel?.(reason);}catch{}}this.promises.clear();this.transactions.clear();this.abortController.abort(reason);this.record("scope-closed",{reason});return true;}
      debug(){return{generation:this.generation,phase:this.phase,projectionEpoch:this.projectionEpoch,closed:this.closed,resourceCount:this.resources.size,promiseCount:this.promises.size,transactionCount:this.transactions.size,trace:this.trace.slice(-160)};}
    }
    const projectStatusDom = () => {
      const session=activeSession,state=session?.machine.state||"IDLE",errorCode=session?.lastError?.code||"",suffix=state==="FAILED"&&errorCode?` · ${errorCode}`:"",text=`${STATUS_TEXT[state]||state}${suffix}`;

      return text;
    };
    const ProjectionTransaction = (() => {
      const trace=[];let lastCommit=null;
      const canonicalRevisionFor=phase=>phase==="LOBBY"?Number(LobbyEngine?.debug?.().revision||0):phase==="IN_BATTLE"||phase==="TERMINAL"?Number(currentGame()?.networkRevision||0):Number(activeSession?.scope?.projectionEpoch||0);
      const commitNavigation=(phase,reason)=>{
        const before=[...document.querySelectorAll(".screen.active")].map(node=>node.id);
        if(phase==="LOBBY"){
          hideCardTooltip();document.querySelectorAll(".screen").forEach(node=>node.classList.remove("active"));document.documentElement.dataset.onlineActiveSurface="LOBBY";document.body.classList.add("online-lobby-open");
        }else if(phase==="IN_BATTLE"){document.documentElement.dataset.onlineActiveSurface="IN_BATTLE";document.body.classList.remove("online-lobby-open");NavigationApplication.request("game");}
        else if(phase==="SETUP"){document.documentElement.dataset.onlineActiveSurface="SETUP";document.body.classList.remove("online-lobby-open");NavigationApplication.request("setup");}
        else if(phase==="TERMINAL")document.documentElement.dataset.onlineActiveSurface="TERMINAL";
        const after=[...document.querySelectorAll(".screen.active")].map(node=>node.id);

        return true;
      };
      const commit=tx=>{const session=activeSession,scope=session?.scope;if(!scope||!scope.valid(tx.identity,tx.phase)||phaseForState(session.machine.state)!==tx.phase&&!(session.machine.state==="RECONNECTING"&&session.resumeState===tx.phase)){counters.staleCallbackDropCount++;scope?.record("projection-drop",{reason:tx.reason,phase:tx.phase});return Object.freeze({...tx,committed:false,dropReason:"IDENTITY_PHASE_OR_EPOCH"});}
        if(scope.transactions.has("restore")&&tx.reason!=="restore-authoritative-apply"&&!tx.allowDuringRestore){scope.record("projection-drop",{reason:tx.reason,phase:tx.phase,dropReason:"RESTORE_INCOMPLETE"});return Object.freeze({...tx,committed:false,dropReason:"RESTORE_INCOMPLETE"});}
        let result=null,phaseCommitOk=true,dropReason=null;projectStatusDom();
        if(tx.phase==="LOBBY"){
          commitNavigation("LOBBY",tx.reason);
          if(session.mode==="BOSS_COOP"){
            const shown=window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.showOnlineLobby?.(true);
            const modal=document.getElementById("modal"),surface=document.querySelector("#modalContent .boss-lobby");
            phaseCommitOk=shown!==false&&!!(surface?.isConnected&&modal?.classList.contains("open"));result={ok:phaseCommitOk,mode:"BOSS_COOP",surface:surface?.className||null};if(!phaseCommitOk)dropReason="BOSS_LOBBY_SURFACE_ACQUISITION_FAILED";
          }else{
            const acquired=LobbyEngine.acquireSurface({reason:`projection:${tx.reason}`,projectionEpoch:tx.projectionEpoch,canonicalRevision:tx.canonicalRevision,surfaceIntent:tx.surfaceIntent||null});
            if(!acquired?.ok){phaseCommitOk=false;dropReason="LOBBY_SURFACE_ACQUISITION_FAILED";result=acquired;}
            else {result=LobbyEngine.project(`projection:${tx.reason}`);phaseCommitOk=!!result?.ok;if(!phaseCommitOk)dropReason=result?.reason||"LOBBY_PROJECTION_NOT_PLAYABLE";}
          }
        }
        else if(tx.phase==="IN_BATTLE"){const g=currentGame(),preserveLocalInput=tx.surfaceIntent==="PRESERVE_LOCAL_INPUT";if(g&&(g.mode==="online"||g?.bossRaid?.online)&&!g.winner){if(!preserveLocalInput)document.getElementById("modal")?.classList.remove("open","online-room-r22","online-room-closing");commitNavigation("IN_BATTLE",tx.reason);if(!preserveLocalInput)render();result=ActionPanelConvergenceOwner?.request?.(`projection:${tx.reason}`,{force:true,networkRevision:Number(g.networkRevision||0)});}else{phaseCommitOk=false;dropReason="BATTLE_CANONICAL_UNAVAILABLE";}}
        else if(tx.phase==="TERMINAL"){commitNavigation("TERMINAL",tx.reason);if(currentGame()?.winner)showEnd();result=true;}
        else if(tx.phase==="SETUP"){document.getElementById("modal")?.classList.remove("open","online-room-r22","online-room-closing");commitNavigation("SETUP",tx.reason);updateDeckSetup();document.documentElement.dataset.onlineSetupProjectionEpoch=String(tx.projectionEpoch||Date.now());result=true;}
        const committed=!!(phaseCommitOk&&scope.valid(tx.identity,tx.phase));lastCommit={...tx,committed,dropReason:committed?null:dropReason||"PHASE_PROJECTION_FAILED",committedAt:Date.now(),result};trace.push(lastCommit);if(trace.length>480)trace.splice(0,trace.length-320);scope.record("projection-commit",{phase:tx.phase,reason:tx.reason,canonicalRevision:tx.canonicalRevision,committed,dropReason:lastCommit.dropR

        else if(tx.phase==="TERMINAL"){commitNavigation("TERMINAL",tx.reason);if(currentGame()?.winner)showEnd();result=true;}
        else if(tx.phase==="SETUP"){document.getElementById("modal")?.classList.remove("open","online-room-r22","online-room-closing");commitNavigation("SETUP",tx.reason);updateDeckSetup();document.documentElement.dataset.onlineSetupProjectionEpoch=String(tx.projectionEpoch||Date.now());result=true;}

      const request=(reason="projection",options={})=>{const session=activeSession,scope=session?.scope;if(!scope||scope.closed){return false;}if(session.kind==="spectator"){return Object.freeze({id:`projection:${++projectionTransactionSerial}`,reason:String(reason),phase:"SESSION",canonicalRevision:0,projectionEpoch:scope.projectionEpoch,createdAt:Date.now(),committed:true,spectatorSidecar:true});}const phase=String(options.phase||phaseForState(session.machine.state));if(scope.phase!==phase)scope.transitionPhase(phase,`projection:${reason}`);const tx=Object.freeze({id:`projection:${++projectionTransactionSerial}`,reason:String(reason),phase,canonicalRevision:Number(options.canonicalRevision??canonicalRevisionFor(phase)),projectionEpoch:scope.projectionEpoch,allowDuringRestore:options.allowDuringRestore===true,surfaceIntent:options.surfaceIntent||null,identity:scope.capture(`projection:${reason}`,phase),createdAt:Date.now()});return commit(tx);};
      const commitDetachedSetup=(reason="result-to-setup")=>{const tx={id:`projection:${++projectionTransactionSerial}`,reason:String(reason),phase:"SETUP",canonicalRevision:0,projectionEpoch:Number(document.documentElement.dataset.onlineSetupProjectionEpoch||0)+1,createdAt:Date.now(),detached:true};document.getElementById("modal")?.classList.remove("open","online-room-r22","online-room-closing");lockOnlineSettings(false);NavigationApplication.request("setup");updateDeckSetup();syncLeagueSelectionSurface();document.documentElement.dataset.onlineSetupProjectionEpoch=String(tx.projectionEpoch);lastCommit={...tx,committed:true,committedAt:Date.now(),result:true};trace.push(lastCommit);return Object.freeze(lastCommit);};
      return Object.freeze({request,commitDetachedSetup,debug:()=>({lastCommit:lastCommit?{...lastCommit}:null,trace:trace.slice(-160)})});
    })();
    const RestoreTransaction = (()=>{
      const trace=[];
      const begin=(session,binding,reason)=>{
        session.routesReady=false;
        const existing=session.scope.transactions.get("restore");
        if(existing){
          Object.assign(existing,{bindingId:binding?.id||existing.bindingId||null,reason:String(reason),restoreRequestId:binding?.restoreRequestId||existing.restoreRequestId||null});
          return existing;
        }
        const row=session.scope.transaction("restore",{bindingId:binding?.id||null,reason:String(reason),phase:session.resumeState||"LOBBY",restoreRequestId:binding?.restoreRequestId||null,transportReady:false,authoritativeApplied:p2p?.role==="host",authoritativeRevision:0});
        trace.push({at:Date.now(),stage:"begin",...row});
        return row;
      };
        const requestAuthoritativePhaseState=(session,phase,reason)=>{
        if(phase==="LOBBY"){
          return LobbyEngine.onTransportReady(reason);
        }
        if(phase==="IN_BATTLE"){
          if(p2p?.role==="host"){
            try{return !!sendAuthoritativeOnlineState([],null,true);}catch{return false;}
          }
          const requested=SnapshotSyncCoordinator.request(reason);
          if(!currentGame())requestOnlineStartReplay(reason);
          return requested;
        }
        return false;
      };
      const finish=(session,binding,detail={})=>{
        if(!session||activeSession!==session||session.scope?.closed)return false;
        const tx=session.scope.transactions.get("restore");
        if(!tx)return session.routesReady;
        const phase=detail.phase||session.resumeState||tx.phase||"LOBBY";
        tx.phase=phase;
        tx.transportReady=detail.transportReady!==false;
        if(p2p?.role==="guest"&&!tx.authoritativeApplied){
          session.routesReady=false;
          session.scope?.record?.("restore-awaiting-fresh-authoritative-state",{bindingId:binding?.id||null,phase,restoreRequestId:tx.restoreRequestId||null});
          requestAuthoritativePhaseState(session,phase,"restore-awaiting-fresh-authoritative-state");
          return false;
        }
        session.scope.transitionPhase(phase,"restore-authoritative-complete");
        session.routesReady=false;
        if(session.machine.state==="RECONNECTING")session.machine.transition(phase,"restore-authoritative-complete",{project:false});
        const restoredGame=currentGame();
        const releaseBusy=!!(restoredGame&&!restoredGame.pendingAnimation&&!restoredGame.pendingClockTurnAdvance&&!onlinePendingCommand&&!pendingSkill);
        if(releaseBusy)busy=false;
        // Projection is synchronous. Open the final eligibility flags in the same task so the
        // action panel is rendered from the committed state, never from the transient reconnect lock.
        session.routesReady=true;
        if(p2p){p2p.reconnecting=false;p2p.connectionInterrupted=false;}
        const projected=ProjectionTransaction.request("restore-authoritative-apply",{phase,canonicalRevision:Number(detail.canonicalRevision||tx.authoritativeRevision||0),surfaceIntent:(detail.inputPreserved===true||tx.inputPreserved===true)?"PRESERVE_LOCAL_INPUT":null});
        session.routesReady=!!projected?.committed;
        if(!session.routesReady){session.lifecycleRestorePending=true;if(p2p){p2p.reconnecting=true;p2p.connectionInterrupted=true;}session.scope?.record?.("restore-projection-failed",{phase,dropReason:projected?.dropReason||null});session.scope.completeTransaction("restore",{state:"FAILED",phase,dropReason:projected?.dropReason||"RESTORE_PROJECTION_FAILED"});if(binding)session.transport?.isolate?.(binding,"restore-projection-failed");else session.transport?.reconnectNow?.("restore-projection-failed");return false;}
        activateOnlineApplicationRoutes(session,phase,"restore-authoritative-complete");
        session.lifecycleRestorePending=false;
        session.lifecycleRestoredEpoch=Number(session.lifecycleEpoch||0);
        session.lifecycleRestoreRequestEpoch=0;
        session.scope.completeTransaction("restore",{phase,projected:!!projected?.committed,authoritativeApplied:true,transportReady:true});
        trace.push({at:Date.now(),stage:"complete",generation:session.generation,bindingId:binding?.id||null,phase,routesReady:session.routesReady,canonicalRevision:Number(detail.canonicalRevision||tx.authoritativeRevision||0)});
        if(session.routesReady&&phase==="LOBBY"&&p2p?.role==="host")LobbyEngine.onTransportReady("restore-host-complete");
        if(session.routesReady&&phase==="IN_BATTLE")requestAuthoritativePhaseState(session,phase,"restore-peer-complete");
        if(session.routesReady&&phase==="IN_BATTLE"&&p2p?.role==="host"&&!pendingSkill)session.scope?.setTimeout?.(()=>scheduleAI(),0,"restore-reschedule-host-turn",{phase:"IN_BATTLE"});
        return projected;
      };
      const transportReady=(session,binding,detail={})=>{
        // A host can already have a provisional game object while the start snapshot is still awaiting guest ACK.
        // Do not promote that half-committed start to IN_BATTLE during reconnect; p2p.battleStarted is the commit gate.
        const phase=session.resumeState==="IN_BATTLE"||!!p2p?.battleStarted?"IN_BATTLE":"LOBBY";
        session.resumeState=phase;
        const tx=session.scope.transactions.get("restore")||begin(session,binding,"transport-ready-without-open-transaction");
        tx.transportReady=true;
        tx.bindingId=binding?.id||tx.bindingId||null;
        if(p2p?.role==="host")tx.authoritativeApplied=true;
        if(p2p?.role==="guest"&&!tx.authoritativeApplied){
          session.routesReady=false;
          requestAuthoritativePhaseState(session,phase,"restore-transport-ready-awaiting-authority");
          return false;
        }
        return finish(session,binding,{...detail,phase,transportReady:true});
      };
      const authoritativeApplied=detail=>{
        const session=activeSession;
        if(!session||session.scope?.closed)return false;
        const tx=session.scope.transactions.get("restore");
        if(!tx)return false;
        tx.authoritativeApplied=true;
        tx.authoritativeRevision=Number(detail?.revision||detail?.canonicalRevision||0);
        tx.authoritativeKind=String(detail?.kind||detail?.phase||"state");
        tx.inputPreserved=detail?.inputPreserved===true;
        trace.push({at:Date.now(),stage:"authoritative-applied",generation:session.generation,phase:detail?.phase||session.resumeState||tx.phase,revision:tx.authoritativeRevision});
        if(!tx.transportReady){session.scope?.record?.("restore-authority-ready-awaiting-transport",{phase:detail?.phase||session.resumeState||tx.phase||"LOBBY",revision:tx.authoritativeRevision,kind:tx.authoritativeKind});return true;}
        return finish(session,session.transport?.activeBinding,{...detail,phase:detail?.phase||session.resumeState||tx.phase||"LOBBY",canonicalRevision:tx.authoritativeRevision,transportReady:true});
      };
      const cancel=(session,reason)=>{session?.scope?.completeTransaction?.("restore",{state:"CANCELLED",reason});session&&(session.routesReady=false);return true;};
      return Object.freeze({begin,transportReady,authoritativeApplied,cancel,debug:()=>trace.slice(-160)});
    })();

    const clean = (value, max = 240) => String(value == null ? "" : value).trim().slice(0, max);
    const randomToken = prefix => {
      try { return randomId(prefix); }
      catch {
        const bytes = new Uint32Array(3); crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
        return `${prefix}-${[...bytes].map(value => value.toString(36)).join("-")}`;
      }
    };
    const makeStableRoomCode = () => {
      const chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
      const values = new Uint32Array(6); crypto.getRandomValues(values);
      return [...values].map(value => chars[value % chars.length]).join("");
    };
    const validateEndpoint = value => {
      const endpoint = clean(value, 500);
      if (!endpoint) return { ok: false, code: "RELAY_ENDPOINT_REQUIRED", detail: "공개 WSS relay 주소를 설정해야 합니다." };
      let url;
      try { url = new URL(endpoint); }
      catch { return { ok: false, code: "RELAY_ENDPOINT_INVALID", detail: "relay 주소 형식이 올바르지 않습니다." }; }
      if (!/^wss?:$/.test(url.protocol)) return { ok: false, code: "RELAY_ENDPOINT_INVALID", detail: "relay 주소는 ws:// 또는 wss:// 여야 합니다." };
      if (location.protocol === "https:" && url.protocol !== "wss:") return { ok: false, code: "RELAY_END
===== transactionId: count=20 =====
--- transactionId: #1 offset=271190 ---
easeOrder: 14, tags: ["electric"], fragmentTrait: true, currentAcceleration: true, alternatingCharge: true, paralyzeImmune: true },
        ),
      ];
      const BASE_IDS = CARDS.filter((c) => c.grade === "2☆" && c.league !== "mega").map((c) => c.id),
        TWO_STAR_IDS = [...BASE_IDS],
        MEGA_IDS = CARDS.filter((c) => c.league === "mega").map((c) => c.id),
        SPECIAL_IDS = [];
      const PLUS_STAR_ELIGIBLE = Object.freeze(new Set(CARDS.filter(c => c.plusStar).map(c => c.id)));
      const PlusStarModifierOwner = Object.freeze({
        eligible: ref => !!card(ref)?.plusStar,
        active: unit => !!unit?.plusStar && !!card(unit)?.plusStar,
        skillModifier(unit, skill) {
          if (!this.active(unit) || !skill?.id) return null;
          return card(unit)?.plusStar?.skills?.[skill.id] || null;
        },
        grade(cardRef, unit = null) {
          return EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(cardRef, unit).rarity;
        },
        name(cardRef, unit = null) {
          return EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(cardRef, unit).displayName;
        },
        cost(unit, skill, fallback = "") {
          const mod = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          return String(mod?.cost ?? fallback ?? skill?.cost ?? "");
        },
        target(unit, skill) {
          const mod = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          return mod?.target || skill?.target || "enemy";
        },
        hitRange(unit, skill) {
          const mod = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          if (Array.isArray(mod?.hitRange)) return [...mod.hitRange];
          if (Array.isArray(skill?.hitRange)) return [...skill.hitRange];
          return null;
        },
        actionSpec(g, teamId, slotIndex, unit, skill, action = null, targets = []) {
          const targetShape = this.target(unit, skill);
          const modifier = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          const canonicalTargets = [...new Map((targets || []).filter(Boolean).map(target => [String(target.uid || `${target.team}:${target.slot}`), target])).values()];
          const targetSlots = canonicalTargets.map(target => Object.freeze({
            team: target.team || other(teamId),
            slot: Number(target.slot),
            uid: target.uid || null,
          }));
          const actionId = String(action?.actionId || action?.commandId || `${g?.battleId || g?.matchId || g?.seed || "battle"}:${g?.turnSerial || 0}:${unit?.uid || "source"}:${skill?.id || "skill"}`);
          return Object.freeze({
            baseSkillId: skill?.id || null,
            sourceInstanceId: unit?.uid || null,
            sourceTeam: teamId,
            sourceSlot: Number(slotIndex),
            deckSlotId: unit?.deckSlotId || unit?.compositionSlotId || `${teamId}:${slotIndex}`,
            plusStar: this.active(unit),
            effectiveCost: this.cost(unit, skill, action?.costOverride ?? skill?.cost ?? ""),
            effectiveTargetShape: targetShape,
            effectiveLegality: this.legal(unit, skill),
            effectiveHitRange: this.hitRange(unit, skill),
            effectiveFollowUpPolicy: modifier?.followUpPolicy || skill?.followUpPolicy || null,
            targetSnapshotPolicy: targetShape === "enemyField" ? "field-slots-at-action-resolution" : "selected-slot",
            targetInstanceIds: Object.freeze(canonicalTargets.map(target => String(target.uid)).filter(Boolean)),
            targetSlots: Object.freeze(targetSlots),
            actionId,
            transactionId: String(action?.transactionId || action?.commandId || actionId),
          });
        },
        description(unit, skill) {
          const mod = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          return String(mod?.description ?? skill?.desc ?? "");
        },
        viewModel(cardRef, unit = null) {
          const vm = EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(cardRef, unit);
          return Object.freeze({ cardId:vm.effectiveCharacterId, plusStar:vm.plusStar, name:vm.displayName, grade:vm.rarity, skills:vm.skills });
        },
        legal(unit, skill) {
          if (!skill) return true;
          const mod = this.skillModifier(unit, skill);
          if (Number.isFinite(Number(skill.useWhenHpAtMost)) && Number(unit?.hp) > Number(skill.useWhenHpAtMost))
            return !!mod?.ignoreUseWhenHpAtMost;
          return true;
        },
      });
      const EffectiveCardPresentationOwner = Object.freeze({
        skillDescription(unit, skill) {
          if (!skill) return "";
          return PlusStarModifierOwner.description(unit, skill);
        },

          const def = card(cardRef), deck = Array.isArray(context.deck) ? context.deck : null;

      function runtimeCardDefinitions() {
        return [...CARDS, ...EXTRA_RUNTIME_SKILL_CONTAINERS.flatMap(x => Array.isArray(x) ? x : Object.values(x || {}))];

      function syncCardRegistry() {
        const defs = runtimeCardDefinitions();
        const signature = defs.map(c => c?.id || "").join("|");
        if (signature === CARD_REGISTRY_SIGNATURE && CARD_REGISTRY.size === defs.filter(Boolean).length) return;
        CARD_REGISTRY.clear();
        for (const def of defs) {
          if (!def?.id) continue;
          if (CARD_REGISTRY.has(def.id)) console.error(`[R41] duplicate card id: ${def.id}`);
          CARD_REGISTRY.set(def.id, def);
        }
        CARD_REGISTRY_SIGNATURE = signature;
      }
      function invokeBossModeHook(name, core, thisArg, args) {
        const hook = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.hooks?.[name];
        if (typeof hook !== "function") return core.apply(thisArg,args);
        return hook((...nextArgs)=>core.apply(thisArg,nextArgs),...args);
      }
      function cardCore(u) {

      function syncRuntimeSkillIndex() {
        syncCardRegistry();

        if (signature === RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX_SIGNATURE) return;

      }

          CARD_REGISTRY.set(def.id, def);
        }

      }
      function invokeBossModeHook(name, core, thisArg, args) {
        const hook = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.hooks?.[name];
        if (typeof hook !== "function") return core.apply(thisArg,args);
        return hook((...nextArgs)=>core.apply(thisArg,nextArgs),...args);
      }

      let RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX_SIGNATURE = "";
      const RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX = new Map();
      function registerRuntimeSkillContainer(container) {
        if (container && !EXTRA_RUNTIME_SKILL_CONTAINERS.includes(container)) EXTRA_RUNTIME_SKILL_CONTAINERS.push(container);
        CARD_REGISTRY_SIGNATURE = "";
        RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX_SIGNATURE = "";

      }
      function syncRuntimeSkillIndex() {
        syncCardRegistry();
        const containers = [...CARDS, ...EXTRA_RUNTIME_SKILL_CONTAINERS.flatMap(x => Array.isArray(x) ? x : Object.values(x || {}))];
        const signature = containers.map(def => `${def?.id || "?"}:${(def?.skills || []).map(sk => sk?.id || "").join(",")}`).join("|");
        if (signature === RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX_SIGNATURE) return;
        RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX.clear();
        for (const def of containers) for (const sk of skillsafeArray(def?.skills)) {
          if (!sk?.id) continue;
          if (!RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX.has(sk.id)) RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX.set(sk.id, []);
          RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX.get(sk.id).push(sk);
        }
        RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX_SIGNATURE = signature;
      }
      function runtimeSkillDefs(skillRef) {
        if (skillRef && typeof skillRef === "object" && skillRef.id) return [skillRef];
        syncRuntimeSkillIndex();
        const id = typeof skillRef === "string" ? skillRef : skillRef?.id;
        return id ? (RUNTIME_SKILL_INDEX.get(id) || []) : [];
      }
      function skillDefHasEffect(skill, effectType) {

      }

      }

      function dealDamageSequence(g, actor, target, amounts, skill, sim = false, baseEffect = {}) {
        const packets = (amounts || []).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);
        actionFaultHit("damage-before");
        let total = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i++) {
          if (actor?.dead || target?.dead) break;
          total += dealDamage(g, actor, target, {
            ...baseEffect,
            amount: packets[i],
            hits: 1,
            _nestedDamageSequence: true,
            _sequenceHitIndex: i + 1,
            _sequenceHitCount: packets.length,
          }, skill, sim);
          if (i === 0) actionFaultHit("first-hit-after");
          if (i + 1 < packets.length && !target?.dead) actionFaultHit("next-hit-before");
        }
        actionFaultHit("damage-after");
        return total;
      }
      function heal(g,u,n,source=null,meta={}) {
        actionFaultHit("healing-before");

          reason:meta?.reason||"heal",effectId:meta?.effectId||null,
          actionId:meta?.actionId||null,transactionId:meta?.transactionId||null,sim:!!meta?.sim,
        });

          const team = teamOf(g, sourceUnit), splash = Math.max(0, Math.floor(missingBefore * splashRatio));

          }
        }
        actionFaultHit("healing-after");

      }
      function createShield(g, u, n, max = 999) {
        n = Math.max(0, combatInt(n));
        max = Math.max(0, combatInt(max));
        u.shield = Math.max(0, combatInt(u.shield));
        let got = Math.min(n, Math.max(0, max - u.shield));
        u.shield += got;
        log(g, `${u.name} 실드 +${got}${got < n ? " (상한 도달)" : ""}.`);
        if (got > 0) queueCombatNotice(g,u,`실드 +${got}`,"shield",{stackAmount:got});
        return got;
      }

      function clearStatuses(g, target, source = null) {

          );

        return true;
      }
      function clearFieldEnergy(g, actor) {
        let at = teamOf(g, actor),
          enemyLost = 0,
          allyLost = 0,
          removed = [];
        for (const t of ["P", "A"])
          for (const u of g.teams[t].field) {

          }

          queueVisual(g, { type: "huveEarthquake", uid: actor.uid, removed });
        }
      }

      function takeTargetEnergyByPlan(target, amount, plan = null, strictPlan = false) {
        let removed = 0, taken = [],
          stores = cardHasCapability(target, "dualActor")
            ? [["beast", target.beastEnergy], ["rider", target.riderEnergy]]
            : [[null, target.energy]];
        const takeFrom = (who, store, index, requested) => {
          if (!store?.[index] || removed + 1e-6 >= amount) return 0;
          const take = Math.min(Number(requested || 0), Number(store[index].a || 0), amount - removed);
          if (!(take > 0)) return 0;
          const color = store[index].c;
          store[index].a -= take; removed += take; taken.push({ c: color, a: take, actor: who });
          if (store[index].a < 0.01) store.splice(index, 1);
          return take;
        };
        if (plan?.length) for (const pick of plan) {
          const pair = stores.find(([who]) => who === (pick.actor ?? null)), store = pair?.[1];
          if (!store) continue;
          const i = store.findIndex(e => e.c === pick.c && e.a + 1e-6 >= Number(pick.a || 0));
          if (i >= 0) takeFrom(pair[0], store, i, Number(pick.a || 0));
          if (removed + 1e-6 >= amount) break;
        }
        if (!strictPlan) for (const [who, store] of stores)
          for (let i = store.length - 1; i >= 0 && removed + 1e-6 < amount; i--)
            takeFrom(who, store, i, amount - removed);
        return taken;
      }
      function removeTargetEnergyByPlan(target, amount, plan = null) {
        return takeTargetEnergyByPlan(target, amount, plan, false).reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.a, 0);
      }
      function restoreTakenEnergy(target, item) {
        const store = cardHasCapability(target, "dualActor")
          ? (item.actor === "rider" ? target.riderEnergy : target.beastEnergy)
          : target.energy;
        store.push({ c: item.c, a: item.a });
      }
      function transferSelectedTargetEnergy(g, actor, target, plan, count = 1) {
        if (!actor || !target || !Array.isArray(plan) || !plan.length) return 0;
        if (typeof circuitBlocksExternal === "function" && circuitBlocksExternal(target, actor)) {
          log(g, `${target.name} 특성 · 프레그먼트: 에너지 강탈 차단.`);
          return 0;
        }
        const selected = plan.slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(count || 1))),
          requested = selected.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item?.a || 0), 0),
          taken = takeTargetEnergyByPlan(target, requested, selected, true);
        let transferred = 0;
        for (const item of taken) {
          if (grantEnergyWithDiscard(g, actor, item.c, item.a)) transferred += item.a;
          else restoreTakenEnergy(target, item);
        }
        if (transferred > 0) {
          actor.energyDisrupt = Number(actor.energyDisrupt || 0) + transferred;
          notifyReactiveEnergyMutation(g, target, false, "에너지 강탈됨");
          notifyReactiveEnergyMutation(g, actor, true, "에너지 강탈");
          log(g, `${actor.name}: ${target.name}에게서 선택한 에너지 ${energyAmountText(transferred)} 강탈.`);
        }
        return transferred;
      }
      function executeMysticBurst(g, actor, target, skill, ctx) {
        let x = ctx?.action?.extraColors || ctx.extraColors || { R: 0, W: 0, B: 0 },
          extraPaid = spendReq(actor, {
            R: x.R,
            W: x.W,
            B: x.B,
            any: 0,
            wb: 0,
          }),
          amount = 100 + 30 * x.R;
        log(
          g,
          `${actor.name}: 미스틱버스트 추가 에너지 ${extraPaid.map((e) => e.c).join("") || "없음"} 지불.`,
        );

        let d = dealDamage(g, actor, target, { amount }, skill, ctx.sim),
          healed = heal(g, actor, 50 * x.W, actor, { reason:"mystic-burst", effectId:skill?.id || "mysticBurst", sim:ctx.sim }),
          rolls = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < x.B && !target.dead; i++) {
          let pool = [
              "burn",
              "paralyze",
              "freeze",
              "poison",
              "slow",
              "traitSeal",
            ],
            st = ctx.sim ? "slow" : g.rng.pick(pool),
            ok = applyStatus(g, target, st, 2, actor);
          rolls.push({ status: st, ok });
        }
        queueVisual(g, {
          type: "mysticBurst",
          uid: actor.uid,
          targetUid: target.uid,
          extraR: x.R,
          extraW: x.W,
          extraB: x.B,
          healed,
          healBlocked: x.W > 0 && healed === 0,
          rolls,
        });
        return d;
      }
      function executeMutualEnergyTrade(g, actor, target, effect, skill, ctx) {
        let action = ctx?.action || ctx,
          requested = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(action.interactionAmount || 0, allEnergy(target)),
          ),
          actionIdentity = String(action?.actionId || action?.commandId || `${g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle"}:${g.turnSerial}:${actor.uid}:${effect?.type || "energy-trade"}`);
        CompositeDamagePresentationOwner.begin(g,{id:`${actionIdentity}:${effect?.type || "energy-trade"}`,skillId:skill?.id||null,sourceInstanceId:actor.uid});
        g.deferWinner = true;
        let attack = dealDamage(
            g,
            actor,
            target,
            { amount: Number(effect?.baseDamage || 0

? effect?.perEnergyDamage ?? 0) * removed,
          enemyDirect = Number(effect?.perEnergyDamage ?? 0) * removed;
        actor.energyDisrupt += removed;

        const sourceLabel = skill?.name || "에너지 교환",
          selfDamage = applyShieldFirstFixedDamage(g, actor, selfDirect, `${sourceLabel} 반동`),
          enemyDamage = applyShieldFirstFixedDamage(g, target, enemyDirect, sourceLabel),
          selfLoss = selfDamage.hpLoss,
          enemyLoss = enemyDamage.hpLoss;

        g.deferWinner = false;
        checkDeath(g, actor, target);
        checkDeath(g, target, actor);
        checkWinner(g);

        log(
          g,
          `${skill?.name || "기술"}: 에너지 ${removed} 제거, ${actor.name} 피해 ${selfDamage.applied}, ${target.name} 피해 ${enemyDamage.applied}.`,
        );
        const compositePresentation = CompositeDamagePresentationOwner.end(g);
        queueVisual(g, {
          type: "mutualEnergyTrade",
          uid: actor.uid,
          targetUid: target.uid,
          amount: removed,
          selfLoss,
          enemyLoss,
          selfTotal:Number(compositePresentation?.totals?.[actor.uid] || selfDamage.applied || 0),
          enemyTotal:Number(compositePresentation?.totals?.[target.uid] || (attack + enemyDamage.applied) || 0),
          compositeDamageGroupId:compositePresentation?.id || null,
          componentEventIds:(compositePresentation?.events || []).map(event=>event.eventId).filter(Boolean),
          selfShieldLoss: selfDamage.shieldLoss,
          enemyShieldLoss: enemyDamage.shieldLoss,
        });
        return attack + enemyDamage.applied;
      }
      function activateFullInvulnerabilityUntilOwnTurn(g, actor, sourceName = "무적") {
        if (!actor || actor.dead) return false;
        actor.fullInvulnerableUntilOwnTurn = true;
        log(g, `${actor.name}: ${sourceName} · 다음 자기 차례 시작 전까지 모든 피해 무효.`);
        queueCombatNotice(g, actor, "전 피해 무적", "immune");
        return true;
      }
      function effectApplyCore(g, actor, target, e, skill, ctx) {
        switch (e.type) {
          case "masterTailwind": {
            let gained = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < Number(e.count || 3); i++) {

                source: "master-tailwind", candidates: ["R","W","B"], amount: 1,
              });
              if (result.gained) gained++;
            }
            log(g, `${actor.name}: 순풍으로 무작위 에너지 ${gained}개 획득.`);
            return gained;

            const hits = ctx.sim ? Math.round((range[0] + range[1]) / 2) : g.rng.int(range[0], range[1]);
            if (skill?.vfxTheme === "leafBlade") {
              const actionIdentity = ctx.action?.actionId || ctx.action?.commandId || `${g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle"}:${g.turnSerial}:${actor.uid}:${skill?.id || "randomMultiHit"}:${target?.uid || "none"}`;
              queueVisual(g, {

                actionId:actionIdentity, transactionId:ctx.action?.commandId || actionIdentity,
                vfxSequenceId:`${actionIdentity}:leaf-blade`, hitCount:hits,
              });
            }
            return dealDamage(g, actor, target, { amount: Number(e.amount || 0), hits }, skill, ctx.sim);
          }
          case "horizonStrike": {
            const actionIdentity = ctx.action?.actionId || ctx.action?.commandId || `${g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle"}:${g.turnSerial}:${actor.uid}:${skill?.id || "horizon"}:${target?.uid || "none"}`;
            const vfxSequenceId = `${actionIdentity}:horizon-strike`;
            queueVisual(g, {
              type: "horizonStrike", uid: actor.uid, targetUid: target?.uid || null,
              sourceInstanceId: actor.uid, targetInstanceId: target?.uid || null,
              sourceTeam: ctx.team, targetTeam: other(ctx.team), sourceSlot: ctx.slot,
              targetSlot: ctx.action?.target?.slot ?? null, skillId: skill?.id || null,
              actionId: actionIdentity, transactionId: ctx.action?.commandId || actionIdentity,
              vfxSequenceId, element: e.element || "sun", status: e.status,
              skillName: skill?.name || "",
            });
            const total = dealDamageSequence(g, actor, target, [40, 100], skill, ctx.sim, { vfxSequenceId });
            if (!target.dead) applyStatus(g, target, e.status, 2, actor, { vfxSequenceId, horizonStatus: true });
            return total;
          }
          case "lunaShard": {
            const enemyTeam = other(ctx.team), targets = g.teams[enemyTeam].field.filter(x => x && !x.dead);
            const energySnapshot = new Map(targets.map(x => [x.uid, allEnergy(x)]));
            const actionIdentity = ctx.action?.actionId || ctx.action?.commandId || `${g.battleId || g.matchId || "battle"}:${g.turnSerial}:${actor.uid}:${skill?.id || "lunaShard"}`;

              const amount = Math.max(0, 80 - 20 * Number(energySnapshot.get(enemy.uid) || 0));

              setAttack(g, enemy, -10);

            return total;

            return 0;
          }

            if (white > black && flowTargets.W) FormFamilyOwner.transition(g, u, flowTargets.W, "시간의 흐름");

          .flatMap((x) => [

          ])
          .filter(Boolean)
          .reduce((n, x) => n + x.damageDealt, 0);

          g.noDamageTurns = 0;

          g.noDamageTurns = streak;

          g.noDamageTurns = streak;

            ? [surface.eligibilityContext.actorTeam, surface.eligibilityContext.actorSlot ?? null]

          const actor = surface.eligibilityContext?.actor || (pair.length ? unitAt(g, pair[0], pair[1]) : null);
          const freezeAction = action => Object.freeze({
            ...(action || {}),
            paymentPlans:Array.isArray(action?.paymentPlans)
              ? Object.freeze(action.paymentPlans.map(plan=>Object.freeze({...plan})))
              : action?.paymentPlans,
            paymentRequirements:action?.paymentRequirements ? Object.freeze({...action.paymentRequirements}) : null,
          });
          const actions = Object.freeze([...(inputProjection.actions || [])].map(freezeAction));
          const actionKeys = Object.freeze(actions.map((action, index) => [
            index,
            action?.type || "",
            action?.skill || "",
            action?.actor || "",
            action?.target?.team || "",
            action?.target?.slot ?? "",
            action?.targetUid || action?.targetInstanceId || "",
            action?.label || "",
            action?.costOverride || action?.displayCost || action?.paymentRequirements?.cost || "",
            action?.actionId || "",
            action?.transactionId || "",
          ].join(":")));
          const pendingInteraction = !!(typeof pendingSkill !== "undefined" && pendingSkill) || !!g?.pendingTargetSelection || !!g?.pendingPayment || !!surface.manualSummon?.pendingSubmitted;
          const canonicalRevision = Number(g?.networkRevision ?? g?.revision ?? 0);
          const authoritativeDigest = String(g?.authoritativeDigest || g?.stateDigest || g?.digest || "");
          const surfaceSignature = surface.manualSummon ? {
            actionId:surface.manualSummon.actionId,
            transactionId:surface.manualSummon.transactionId,
            sourceInstanceId:surface.manualSummon.sourceInstanceId,
            candidateIdentity:surface.manualSummon.candidateIdentity,
            pendingSubmitted:surface.manualSummon.pendingSubmitted,
            submitState:surface.manualSummon.submitState,
          } : surface.boss ? {online:surface.boss.online,localControl:surface.boss.localControl} : null;
          const signature = JSON.stringify({
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            surfaceSignature,
            currentActorUid:inputProjection.currentActorUid || null,
            currentActorTeam:inputProjection.currentActorCanonicalTeam || null,
            localTeam:inputProjection.canonicalLocalTeam || null,
            canAct:inputProjection.canAct === true,
            reasons:[...(inputProjection.reasons || [])],
            busy:!!busy,
            pendingInteraction,
            pendingActionId:inputProjection.pendingActionId || surface.manualSummon?.actionId || null,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
            actionKeys,
          });
          return Object.freeze({
            game:g,
            projection:inputProjection,
            actions,
            actionKeys,
            signature,
            canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeDigest,
            turnSerial:Number(g?.turnSerial || 0),
            battleId:g?.battleId || g?.matchId || null,
            actor,
            actorTeam:pair[0] ?? null,
            actorSlot:pair[1] ?? null,
            surfaceKind:surface.kind,
            manualSummon:surface.manualSummon,
            boss:surface.boss,
            pendingInteraction,
            terminal:!!(g?.winner || g?.ended),
          });
        },
      });
      function renderBaseActions(viewModel = null) {
        const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
        let box = $("#actionButtons"),
          t = panelView.actorTeam,
          s = panelView.actorSlot,
          u = panelView.actor;
        const inputProjection = panelView.projection;
        box.innerHTML = "";
        let dual =
          cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") && game.dualActorController?.uid === u.uid
            ? ` · ${game.dualActorController.actor === "beast" ? "빙마 행동" : "기수 행동"}`
            : "";
        $("#actionTitle").textContent = game.winner
          ? "전투 종료"
          : busy
            ? (game?.mode === "online"
                ? (onlineAutoCommandActive
                    ? "예약 행동 처리 중…"
                    : (typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand
                        ? "행동 동기화 중…"
                        : "온라인 처리 중…"))
                : "AI 계산 중…")
          : inputProjection.ownsCurrentActor
              ? u && !u.dead
                ? `${u.name}${dual}`
                : `슬롯 ${s + 1} 출전`
              : game.mode === "online"
                ? "상대 행동 대기 중…"
                : "자동 진행 중";
        $("#actionHint").textContent = pendingSkill
          ? "유효한 대상을 선택하세요."
          : game.fieldEffect?.type === "grand"
            ? `그랜드필드: ${game.fieldEffect.team === "P" ? "플레이어" : "AI"} 진영 소유`
            : "";
        if (game.winner) {
          box.innerHTML =
            '<button id="sameRestart" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">같은 조건으로 재시작</button><button data-nav="setup" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">메인 메뉴</button>';

          box.querySelector("[data-nav]").onclick = () => NavigationApplication.request("setup");
          return;
        }
        let controllable = inputProjection.ownsCurrentActor,
          acts = inputProjection.actions;
        if (!acts.length) {
          $("#actionHint").textContent = "행동 불가 · 자동으로 턴을 넘깁니다.";
          return;
        }
        if (busy || !controllable || !inputProjection.canAct) return;
        if (!u || u.dead) {

              const executed = execute(game, t, s, a);
              if (executed) afterAction();

          );
          return;
        }
        let speedDelay = acts.find((a) => a.type === "speedDelay");

            serial = game.turnSerial;

            "speed-delay",
            true ? 550 : 0,
            () => {
              if (
                !game ||
                game.winner ||
                game.token !== token ||
                game.turnSerial !== serial
              ) {
                busy = false;
                return;
              }

        }
        let scheduled = acts.find((a) => a.type === "scheduled");

      function submitManualSummonAction(a){
        const cur=normalizePendingSummonTurns(game); if(!cur||!a)return;
        const {q,u,owner}=cur;
        if(a.actionId&&a.actionId!==q.actionId)return;
        if(a.transactionId&&a.transactionId!==q.transactionId)return;
        if(Number.isFinite(Number(a.authoritativeRevision))&&Number(a.authoritativeRevision)!==Number(game.networkRevision||0))return;
        const canonicalAction={...a,actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,sourceInstanceId:u.uid};
        if(game.mode==="online"&&p2p?.role==="guest"&&q.team===onlineSide&&!p2p.applyingRemoteAction){
          if(onlinePendingCommand||q.submitState!=="AWAITING_INPUT")return;
          q.submitState="SUBMITTED";
          q.submittedAction={type:canonicalAction.type||null,skill:canonicalAction.skill||null,targetUid:canonicalAction.targetUid||null,paymentPlan:canonicalAction.paymentPlan?{...canonicalAction.paymentPlan}:null};
          beginOnlinePendingCommand({
            type:"actionRequest",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:"PVP",commandKind:"summon",
            matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,
            expectedTurnSerial:game.turnSerial,expectedTurn:game.turn,team:q.team,summonUid:u.uid,ownerUid:owner.uid,
            action:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(canonicalAction))
          });
          return;
        }
        q.submitState="COMMITTING";
        if(applyManualSummonAction(game,q.team,u.uid,canonicalAction))afterAction();
        else {q.submitState="AWAITING_INPUT";renderActions("manual-summon-rejected");}
      }
      function openSummonGatherChoice(base){
        const cur=normalizePendingSummonTurns(game);if(!cur||!base||base.actionId!==cur.q.actionId)return;
        const {q,u}=cur,col=base.gather?.color,amt=Number(base.gather?.amount||1),max=Number(base.gather?.max||card(u).maxEnergy||4),store=energyStore(u);
        if(totalEnergy(u)+amt<=max+.001){submitManualSummonAction({type:'summonGather',color:col});return;}
        const need=totalEnergy(u)+amt-max,chosen=new Set(),root=$('#modalContent');
        root.innerHTML=`<h2>${u.name} · 에너지 모으기</h2><p>이번 수급은 <b>${col}${amt===.5?'½':''}</b>입니다. 합계 ${energyAmountText(need)} 이상 버릴 에너지를 선택하세요.</p><div id="summonDiscardList" class="choice-list"></div><p id="summonDiscardTotal" class="tiny"></p><div class="choice-list"><button id="summonDiscardNew">새 에너지 버리기</button><button id="summonKeepNew" class="primary" disabled>선택한 에너지 버리고 획득</button><button id="summonGatherCancel">취소</button></div>`;
        const list=$('#summonDiscardList'),label=$('#summonDiscardTotal'),keep=$('#summonKeepNew');
        const refresh=()=>{const total=[...chosen].reduce((n,i)=>n+(store[i]?.a||0),0);label.textContent=`선택 ${energyAmountText(total)} / 필요 ${energyAmountText(need)}`;keep.disabled=total+1e-6<need;};
        store.forEach((e,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className=`e ${e.c}`;b.textContent=`${e.c}${e.a===.5?'½':''}`;b.onclick=()=>{chosen.has(i)?chosen.delete(i):chosen.add(i);b.classList.toggle('chosen',chosen.has(i));refresh();};list.appendChild(b);});refresh();
        $('#summonDiscardNew').onclick=()=>{$('#modal').classList.remove('open');submitManualSummonAction({type:'summonGather',color:col,discardNew:true});};
        keep.onclick=()=>{$('#modal').classList.remove('open');submitManualSummonAction({type:'summonGather',color:col,replaceIndices:[...chosen]});};
        $('#summonGatherCancel').onclick=()=>$('#modal').classList.remove('open');
        $('#modal').classList.add('open');
      }
      function chooseSummonSkillPayment(base){
        const cur=normalizePendingSummonTurns(game);if(!cur||!base||base.actionId!==cur.q.actionId)return;
        const cost=base.paymentRequirements?.cost||'',plans=Array.isArray(base.paymentPlans)?base.paymentPlans:[];
        if(!plans.length)return;
        if(plans.length===1){submitManualSummonAction({...base,paymentPlan:plans[0]});return;}
        openChoice(`<h2>${cur.u.name} · ${card(cur.u).skills.find(x=>x.id===base.skill)?.name}</h2><p>지불할 에너지 색을 선택하세요.</p>`,plans.map(plan=>({label:`R ${energyAmountText(plan.R)} / W ${energyAmountText(plan.W)} / B ${energyAmountText(plan.B)}`,fn:()=>submitManualSummonAction({...base,paymentPlan:plan})})));
      }

      function renderManualSummonActions(viewModel){
        const box=$('#actionButtons'),surface=viewModel?.manualSummon;
        box.innerHTML='';

        if(!viewModel.projection.canAct||surface.pendingSubmitted)return;
        for(const a of surface.actions){
          actionButton(box,a.label,()=>a.type==='summonGather'?openSummonGatherChoice(a):chooseSummonSkillPayment(a));
        }
      }

      function chooseSummonAIAction(g,t,u){
        const opts=summonActionOptions(g,t,u).filter(a=>a.type==='summonSkill');
        let pick=null;
        if(cardCapability(u,'summonBehavior','')==='support'){
          const injured=summonTargetRefs(g,t,true).map(x=>x.u).filter(x=>x.hp<x.maxHp).sort((a,b)=>(b.maxHp-b.hp)-(a.maxHp-a.hp))[0];
          const enemy=summonTargetRefs(g,t,false).map(x=>x.u).filter(x=>allEnergy(x)>.001).sort((a,b)=>allEnergy(b)-allEnergy(a))[0];
          if(enemy)pick=opts.find(a=>actionSkillHasRuntimeKind(a, "summonEnergyPeck")&&a.targetUid===enemy.uid);
          if(!pick&&injured)pick=opts.find(a=>actionSkillHasRuntimeKind(a, "summonHeal")&&a.targetUid===injured.uid);
        }else{
          const enemy=bestSummonEnemy(g,t);
          pick=enemy&&opts.find(a=>a.targetUid===enemy.uid);
        }
        if(!pick)return {type:'summonGather'};
        const cost=summonSkillCost(g,t,u,pick.skill),plan=cost?findPaymentPlan(g,t,u,cost):{R:0,W:0,B:0};
        return {...pick,paymentPlan:plan};
      }

      function rawReqFromCost(cost) {
        const tokens = costTokens(cost), req = { R:0, W:0, B:0, any:0, wb:0 };
        for (const x of tokens) {
          if (x === "*") req.any++;
          else if (x === "WorB") req.wb++;
          else req[x]++;
        }
        return { req, tokens, text: tokens.join("") };
      }
      function circuitIgnoresCostChanges(u) {
        return !!(u && cardHasCapability(u, "fragmentTrait") && traitsActive(u));
      }

      function rawReqFromCost(cost) {
        const tokens = costTokens(cost), req = { R:0, W:0, B:0, any:0, wb:0 };
        for (const x of tokens) {
          if (x === "*") req.any++;
          else if (x === "WorB") req.wb++;
          else req[x]++;
        }
        return { req, tokens, text: tokens.join("") };
      }

  const ACTION_FAULT_PHASES = Object.freeze([
    "validation-after","draft-after","payment-before","payment-after","energy-proposal-before","energy-proposal-after","rng-before","rng-after","damage-before","first-hit-after","next-hit-before","damage-after","healing-before","healing-after","status-before","status-after","schedule-before","schedule-after","death-before","death-after","replacement-pending-after","victory-before","victory-after","terminal-pending-after","terminal-commit-before","event-publication-before","remote-ack-before","render-before","vfx-before","audio-before"
  ]);
  const ActionFaultInjector = (()=>{
    let armed=null, hits=[];
    return Object.freeze({
      phases:()=>[...ACTION_FAULT_PHASES],
      arm:phase=>{if(!ACTION_FAULT_PHASES.includes(phase))throw new Error(`unknown fault phase ${phase}`);armed=phase;hits=[];},
      clear:()=>{armed=null;hits=[];},
      hit(phase){hits.push(phase);if(armed===phase){armed=null;const error=new Error(`CP32_FAULT:${phase}`);error.code="CP32_FAULT_INJECTED";error.phase=phase;throw error;}},
      debug:()=>({armed,hits:[...hits]}),
    });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"ActionFaultInjector",{value:ActionFaultInjector,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const PresentationFailureChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ presentationSequence:events.length+1, timestamp:Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if(events.length>512)events.splice(0,events.length-512);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x=>({...x}));
    const clear = () => { events.length=0; };
    return Object.freeze({emit,snapshot,clear});
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"PresentationFailureChannel",{value:PresentationFailureChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if (events.length > 2048) events.splice(0, events.length - 2048);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const forAction = actionId => events.filter(x => x.actionId === actionId).map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"TransactionAuditChannel",{value:TransactionAuditChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});
  const ActionTransactionManager = (() => {
    const committed = new Map(), failed = new Map();
    const begin = (g,t,s,action) => {
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      if (seen.has(actionId) || rejected.has(actionId)) return { duplicate:true, actionId, terminalStatus:seen.has(actionId)?"committed":"failed" };
      const transaction = { transactionId:IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId), actionId, teamId:t, slot:s, action, startedAt:now(), eventSequenceAtStart:Number(g.eventSequence||0), snapshot:snapshotState(g) };
      g.pendingTransaction = transaction; g.pendingAction = action; g.phase = "EXECUTING_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{transactionId:transaction.transactionId,actionId,actionType:action?.type}});
      return transaction;
    };
    const commit = (g,tx) => {
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); seen.add(tx.actionId); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_COMMITTED",{teamId:tx.teamId,payload:{transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,durationMs:now()-tx.startedAt}});
    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const diffUnits = (before, g, causedByEventId=null, eventSequenceAtStart=0) => {
    EntityRegistry.sync(g);
    const previous = new Map();

    } catch (error) { console.error("[R48] diff snapshot parse failed",error); }
    for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities || {})) {
      const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid, old = previous.get(id); if (!old) continue;
      const common = { recipientEntityId:id, teamId:typeof teamOf === "function" ? teamOf(g,unit) : null, causedByEventId };
      const hasDetailedDamage = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ev.type === "DAMAGE_APPLIED" &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&

        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ["HEAL_APPLIED","HEALING_APPLIED"].includes(ev.type) &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      if (Number(unit.hp)!==Number(old.hp) && !((unit.hp<old.hp&&hasDetailedDamage)||(unit.hp>old.hp&&hasDetailedHeal))) {
        if (unit.hp < old.hp) emitDamageAppliedEvent(g, null, unit, {
          damageType:"stateDiff", before:{hp:old.hp,shield:old.shield}, after:{hp:unit.hp,shield:unit.shield},
          amount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp), actionId:g?.pendingTransaction?.actionId || null,
        });

      }

      const oldStatus=stableText(old.statuses||{}),newStatus=stableText(unit.statuses||{});

      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
