  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

  const PresentationFailureChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ presentationSequence:events.length+1, timestamp:Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if(events.length>512)events.splice(0,events.length-512);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x=>({...x}));
    const clear = () => { events.length=0; };
    return Object.freeze({emit,snapshot,clear});
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"PresentationFailureChannel",{value:PresentationFailureChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);

    };

    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"TransactionAuditChannel",{value:TransactionAuditChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});
  const ActionTransactionManager = (() => {
    const committed = new Map(), failed = new Map();
    const begin = (g,t,s,action) => {
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});

    const commit = (g,tx) => {

      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";

    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const TurnEngine = (() => {

        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",

      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      if (seen.has(actionId) || rejected.has(actionId)) return { duplicate:true, actionId, terminalStatus:seen.has(actionId)?"committed":"failed" };
      const transaction = { transactionId:IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId), actionId, teamId:t, slot:s, action, startedAt:now(), eventSequenceAtStart:Number(g.eventSequence||0), snapshot:snapshotState(g) };
      g.pendingTransaction = transaction; g.pendingAction = action; g.phase = "EXECUTING_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{transactionId:transaction.transactionId,actionId,actionType:action?.type}});
      return transaction;
    };
    const commit = (g,tx) => {
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); seen.add(tx.actionId); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_COMMITTED",{teamId:tx.teamId,payload:{transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,durationMs:now()-tx.startedAt}});
    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const diffUnits = (before, g, causedByEventId=null, eventSequenceAtStart=0) => {
    EntityRegistry.sync(g);
    const previous = new Map();
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(before.raw);
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const zone of ["field","bench","graveyard","summons"]) for (const unit of raw.teams?.[team]?.[zone] || []) if (unit) previous.set(unit.entityId || unit.uid,unit);
    } catch (error) { console.error("[R48] diff snapshot parse failed",error); }
    for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities || {})) {
      const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid, old = previous.get(id); if (!old) continue;
      const common = { recipientEntityId:id, teamId:typeof teamOf === "function" ? teamOf(g,unit) : null, causedByEventId };
      const hasDetailedDamage = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ev.type === "DAMAGE_APPLIED" &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      const hasDetailedHeal = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ["HEAL_APPLIED","HEALING_APPLIED"].includes(ev.type) &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      if (Number(unit.hp)!==Number(old.hp) && !((unit.hp<old.hp&&hasDetailedDamage)||(unit.hp>old.hp&&hasDetailedHeal))) {
        if (unit.hp < old.hp) emitDamageAppliedEvent(g, null, unit, {
          damageType:"stateDiff", before:{hp:old.hp,shield:old.shield}, after:{hp:unit.hp,shield:unit.shield},
          amount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp), actionId:g?.pendingTransaction?.actionId || null,
        });
        else if(g?.pendingTransaction&&g.phase==="EXECUTING_ACTION") GenericBattleStatisticsOwner.recordHealing(g,{source:null,target:unit,requestedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),actualAppliedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),reason:"action-state-diff",effectId:"actionStateDiff",actionId:g.pendingTransaction.actionId||null,transactionId:g.pendingTransaction.transactionId||null});
      }
      if (Number(unit.shield)!==Number(old.shield)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SHIELD_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.shield,after:unit.shield}});
      const oldStatus=stableText(old.statuses||{}),newStatus=stableText(unit.statuses||{});
      if(oldStatus!==newStatus) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"STATUS_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.statuses||{},after:unit.statuses||{}}});
      const oldEnergy=(old.energy||[]).map(e=>({energyId:e.energyId,c:e.c,a:e.a})),newEnergy=EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit);
      if(stableText(oldEnergy)!==stableText(newEnergy)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENERGY_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:oldEnergy,after:newEnergy}});
      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
    reconcile(g) {
      StateFactory.decorate(g); const deaths=[];
      for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities||{})) if (Number(unit.hp)<=0 && !unit.dead && typeof checkDeath === "function") { checkDeath(g,unit,null); deaths.push(unit.entityId||unit.uid); }
      EntityRegistry.sync(g); return deaths;
    },
    alive:(g,team)=>typeof aliveUnits === "function" ? aliveUnits(g.teams[team]) : []
  });

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

  // Live authoritative actions use the transaction boundary. Guest request submission and simulation do not mutate authority state.
  const isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution = (g,t,s,opt={}) => {
    if (opt?.sim || !g || g !== currentGame() || !g?.bossRaid?.online) return false;
    if (p2p?.role !== "guest" || p2p?.applyingRemoteAction || t !== "P") return false;
    const active = g.order?.[g.turn];
    if (!active || active[0] !== "P" || Number(active[1]) !== Number(s)) return false;
    if (!window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.localControlsCurrent?.()) return false;
    const unit = unitAt(g,t,s);
    return unit ? unit.bossOwner === "guest" : g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s] === "guest";
  };
  const classifyR48Execution = (g,t,s,action,opt={}) => {
    const live = !opt?.sim && g && (g === currentGame() || opt?.r48Live === true);
    if (!live) return "simulation";
    const standardGuestRequest = g === currentGame() && g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid &&
      p2p?.role === "guest" && !p2p?.applyingRemoteAction && t === onlineSide;
    const bossGuestRequest = isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution(g,t,s,opt);
    return standardGuestRequest || bossGuestRequest ? "request-only" : "authoritative-execution";
  };

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if (events.length > 2048) events.splice(0, events.length - 2048);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const forAction = actionId => events.filter(x => x.actionId === actionId).map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();

        ["HEAL_APPLIED","HEALING_APPLIED"].includes(ev.type) &&
        ev.recipientEntityId === id &&
        ev.payload?.detailed === true
      );
      if (Number(unit.hp)!==Number(old.hp) && !((unit.hp<old.hp&&hasDetailedDamage)||(unit.hp>old.hp&&hasDetailedHeal))) {
        if (unit.hp < old.hp) emitDamageAppliedEvent(g, null, unit, {
          damageType:"stateDiff", before:{hp:old.hp,shield:old.shield}, after:{hp:unit.hp,shield:unit.shield},
          amount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp), actionId:g?.pendingTransaction?.actionId || null,
        });
        else if(g?.pendingTransaction&&g.phase==="EXECUTING_ACTION") GenericBattleStatisticsOwner.recordHealing(g,{source:null,target:unit,requestedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),actualAppliedAmount:Math.abs(unit.hp-old.hp),reason:"action-state-diff",effectId:"actionStateDiff",actionId:g.pendingTransaction.actionId||null,transactionId:g.pendingTransaction.transactionId||null});
      }
      if (Number(unit.shield)!==Number(old.shield)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SHIELD_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.shield,after:unit.shield}});
      const oldStatus=stableText(old.statuses||{}),newStatus=stableText(unit.statuses||{});
      if(oldStatus!==newStatus) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"STATUS_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:old.statuses||{},after:unit.statuses||{}}});
      const oldEnergy=(old.energy||[]).map(e=>({energyId:e.energyId,c:e.c,a:e.a})),newEnergy=EnergyEngine.snapshot(unit);
      if(stableText(oldEnergy)!==stableText(newEnergy)) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENERGY_CHANGED",{...common,payload:{before:oldEnergy,after:newEnergy}});
      if(!old.dead&&unit.dead) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ENTITY_DIED",{...common,payload:{cardId:unit.card}});
    }
  };

  const DeathEngine = Object.freeze({
    reconcile(g) {
      StateFactory.decorate(g); const deaths=[];
      for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities||{})) if (Number(unit.hp)<=0 && !unit.dead && typeof checkDeath === "function") { checkDeath(g,unit,null); deaths.push(unit.entityId||unit.uid); }
      EntityRegistry.sync(g); return deaths;
    },
    alive:(g,team)=>typeof aliveUnits === "function" ? aliveUnits(g.teams[team]) : []
  });

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

  // Live authoritative actions use the transaction boundary. Guest request submission and simulation do not mutate authority state.
  const isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution = (g,t,s,opt={}) => {
    if (opt?.sim || !g || g !== currentGame() || !g?.bossRaid?.online) return false;
    if (p2p?.role !== "guest" || p2p?.applyingRemoteAction || t !== "P") return false;
    const active = g.order?.[g.turn];
    if (!active || active[0] !== "P" || Number(active[1]) !== Number(s)) return false;
    if (!window.__redTimeoraBossRaid?.localControlsCurrent?.()) return false;
    const unit = unitAt(g,t,s);
    return unit ? unit.bossOwner === "guest" : g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s] === "guest";
  };
  const classifyR48Execution = (g,t,s,action,opt={}) => {
    const live = !opt?.sim && g && (g === currentGame() || opt?.r48Live === true);
    if (!live) return "simulation";
    const standardGuestRequest = g === currentGame() && g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid &&
      p2p?.role === "guest" && !p2p?.applyingRemoteAction && t === onlineSide;
    const bossGuestRequest = isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution(g,t,s,opt);
    return standardGuestRequest || bossGuestRequest ? "request-only" : "authoritative-execution";
  };
  function actionTransactionExecuteStage(next,g,t,s,action,opt={}) {
      const disposition = classifyR48Execution(g,t,s,action,opt);
      if (disposition !== "authoritative-execution") return next(g,t,s,action,opt);
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      if (g.ended || g.winner) return false;
      if (action && (action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "sequence")) {
        const scheduled = ensureScheduledActionIdentity(g, unitAt(g, t, s));
        if (scheduled) {
          action.scheduledActionId ||= scheduled.scheduledActionId;
          action.sourceInstanceId ||= scheduled.sourceInstanceId;
          action.actionId ||= scheduledExecutionId(g, scheduled);
        }
      }
      const tx = ActionTransactionManager.begin(g,t,s,action);
      if (tx.duplicate) return false;
      const lock = InputLockManager.lock("action-transaction",tx.transactionId);
      try {
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("validation-after");
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("draft-after");
        if (action?.type === "skill") {
          ActionFaultInjector.hit("payment-before");
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SKILL_SELECTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,copySkillId:action.copySkill||null}});
          if (action.target) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TARGET_SELECTED",{teamId:action.target.team,targetSlotId:IdFactory.slot(action.target.team,"field",action.target.slot),recipientEntityId:action.target.uid||null,payload:{target:action.target}});
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"PAYMENT_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,paymentPlan:action.paymentPlan||null}});
        }
        const result = next(g,t,s,action,opt);
        if (!result) { ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"action-rejected"); return false; }
        const deaths = DeathEngine.reconcile(g);
        StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("event-publication-before");
        const commitEvent = BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_EXECUTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:tx.snapshot ? null : g.activeEntityId,payload:{actionId:tx.actionId,actionType:action?.type,deaths}});
        diffUnits(tx.snapshot,g,commitEvent?.eventId||null,tx.eventSequenceAtStart);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("remote-ack-before");
        ActionTransactionManager.commit(g,tx);
        StateValidator.validate(g);
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        const isGradeValidation = error?.code === "INVALID_CARD_GRADE";
        if (!isGradeValidation) console.error("[R48 ActionEngine] execution failed",{battleId:g?.battleId,team:t,slot:s,action,error});
        const sourceEntityId = tx.snapshot?.activeEntityId || g.activeEntityId || null;
        ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"exception",error);
        if (isGradeValidation) {
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"GRADE_VALIDATION_FAILED",{
            teamId:t,
            sourceEntityId,
            recipientEntityId:action?.target?.uid || null,
            payload:{ ...(error.gradeValidationDetail || {}), message:String(error.message || error) },
          });
        }
        throw error;
      } finally { InputLockManager.unlock(lock); }
  }

  Object.defineProperty(window,"PresentationFailureChannel",{value:PresentationFailureChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});

  const TransactionAuditChannel = (() => {
    const events = [];
    const emit = record => {
      const frozen = Object.freeze({ auditSequence: events.length + 1, timestamp: Date.now(), ...record });
      events.push(frozen);
      if (events.length > 2048) events.splice(0, events.length - 2048);
      return frozen;
    };
    const snapshot = () => events.map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const forAction = actionId => events.filter(x => x.actionId === actionId).map(x => ({ ...x }));
    const clear = () => { events.length = 0; };
    return Object.freeze({ emit, snapshot, forAction, clear });
  })();
  Object.defineProperty(window,"TransactionAuditChannel",{value:TransactionAuditChannel,writable:false,configurable:false});
  const ActionTransactionManager = (() => {
    const committed = new Map(), failed = new Map();
    const begin = (g,t,s,action) => {
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      const actionId = action?.actionId || action?._r48ActionId || IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId);
      if (action && typeof action === "object" && !action._r48ActionId) Object.defineProperty(action,"_r48ActionId",{value:actionId,configurable:true});
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      if (seen.has(actionId) || rejected.has(actionId)) return { duplicate:true, actionId, terminalStatus:seen.has(actionId)?"committed":"failed" };
      const transaction = { transactionId:IdFactory.transaction(g.battleId), actionId, teamId:t, slot:s, action, startedAt:now(), eventSequenceAtStart:Number(g.eventSequence||0), snapshot:snapshotState(g) };
      g.pendingTransaction = transaction; g.pendingAction = action; g.phase = "EXECUTING_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{transactionId:transaction.transactionId,actionId,actionType:action?.type}});
      return transaction;
    };
    const commit = (g,tx) => {
      const seen = committed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); seen.add(tx.actionId); committed.set(g.battleId,seen);
      g.pendingTransaction=null; g.pendingAction=null; g.pendingTargetSelection=null; g.pendingPayment=null;
      g.phase = g.winner ? "BATTLE_ENDED" : "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_TRANSACTION_COMMITTED",{teamId:tx.teamId,payload:{transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,durationMs:now()-tx.startedAt}});
    };
    const rollback = (g,tx,reason,error=null) => {
      restoreState(g,tx.snapshot);
      InputLockManager.unlock("payment"); InputLockManager.unlock("target-selection"); InputLockManager.unlock("action-transaction");
      const rejected = failed.get(g.battleId) || new Set(); rejected.add(tx.actionId); failed.set(g.battleId,rejected);
      const rollbackEvent = TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:"ACTION_TRANSACTION_ROLLED_BACK",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      TransactionAuditChannel.emit({type:reason === "action-rejected" ? "ACTION_COMMAND_REJECTED" : "ACTION_COMMAND_FAILED",battleId:g.battleId,teamId:tx.teamId,transactionId:tx.transactionId,actionId:tx.actionId,reason,error:error?String(error):null});
      try { if (typeof p2p !== "undefined") { p2p.remotePending = null; p2p.terminalPending = null; } } catch {}
      return rollbackEvent;
    };
    const clearBattle = id => {
      committed.delete(id); failed.delete(id);
      const g = currentGame();
      if (g?.battleId === id) {
        g.pendingTransaction = null;
        g.pendingAction = null;
        g.pendingTargetSelection = null;
        g.pendingPayment = null;
        g.pendingAnimation = null;
        if (!g.winner) g.phase = "WAITING_FOR_ACTION";
      }
      return true;
    };
    return Object.freeze({ begin, commit, rollback, clearBattle });
  })();

  const diffUnits = (before, g, causedByEventId=null, eventSequenceAtStart=0) => {
    EntityRegistry.sync(g);
    const previous = new Map();
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(before.raw);
      for (const team of ["P","A"]) for (const zone of ["field","bench","graveyard","summons"]) for (const unit of raw.teams?.[team]?.[zone] || []) if (unit) previous.set(unit.entityId || unit.uid,unit);
    } catch (error) { console.error("[R48] diff snapshot parse failed",error); }
    for (const unit of Object.values(g.entities || {})) {
      const id = unit.entityId || unit.uid, old = previous.get(id); if (!old) continue;
      const common = { recipientEntityId:id, teamId:typeof teamOf === "function" ? teamOf(g,unit) : null, causedByEventId };
      const hasDetailedDamage = (g.eventLog || []).some(ev =>
        Number(ev.sequence || 0) > Number(eventSequenceAtStart || 0) &&
        ev.type === "DAMAGE_APPLIED" &&

      p2p?.role === "guest" && !p2p?.applyingRemoteAction && t === onlineSide;
    const bossGuestRequest = isBossGuestRequestOnlyExecution(g,t,s,opt);
    return standardGuestRequest || bossGuestRequest ? "request-only" : "authoritative-execution";
  };
  function actionTransactionExecuteStage(next,g,t,s,action,opt={}) {
      const disposition = classifyR48Execution(g,t,s,action,opt);
      if (disposition !== "authoritative-execution") return next(g,t,s,action,opt);
      StateFactory.decorate(g);
      if (g.ended || g.winner) return false;
      if (action && (action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "sequence")) {
        const scheduled = ensureScheduledActionIdentity(g, unitAt(g, t, s));
        if (scheduled) {
          action.scheduledActionId ||= scheduled.scheduledActionId;
          action.sourceInstanceId ||= scheduled.sourceInstanceId;
          action.actionId ||= scheduledExecutionId(g, scheduled);
        }
      }
      const tx = ActionTransactionManager.begin(g,t,s,action);
      if (tx.duplicate) return false;
      const lock = InputLockManager.lock("action-transaction",tx.transactionId);
      try {
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("validation-after");
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("draft-after");
        if (action?.type === "skill") {
          ActionFaultInjector.hit("payment-before");
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"SKILL_SELECTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,copySkillId:action.copySkill||null}});
          if (action.target) BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"TARGET_SELECTED",{teamId:action.target.team,targetSlotId:IdFactory.slot(action.target.team,"field",action.target.slot),recipientEntityId:action.target.uid||null,payload:{target:action.target}});
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"PAYMENT_STARTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:g.activeEntityId,payload:{skillId:action.skill,paymentPlan:action.paymentPlan||null}});
        }
        const result = next(g,t,s,action,opt);
        if (!result) { ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"action-rejected"); return false; }
        const deaths = DeathEngine.reconcile(g);
        StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("event-publication-before");
        const commitEvent = BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"ACTION_EXECUTED",{teamId:t,sourceEntityId:tx.snapshot ? null : g.activeEntityId,payload:{actionId:tx.actionId,actionType:action?.type,deaths}});
        diffUnits(tx.snapshot,g,commitEvent?.eventId||null,tx.eventSequenceAtStart);
        ActionFaultInjector.hit("remote-ack-before");
        ActionTransactionManager.commit(g,tx);
        StateValidator.validate(g);
        return true;
      } catch (error) {
        const isGradeValidation = error?.code === "INVALID_CARD_GRADE";
        if (!isGradeValidation) console.error("[R48 ActionEngine] execution failed",{battleId:g?.battleId,team:t,slot:s,action,error});
        const sourceEntityId = tx.snapshot?.activeEntityId || g.activeEntityId || null;
        ActionTransactionManager.rollback(g,tx,"exception",error);
        if (isGradeValidation) {
          BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"GRADE_VALIDATION_FAILED",{
            teamId:t,
            sourceEntityId,
            recipientEntityId:action?.target?.uid || null,
            payload:{ ...(error.gradeValidationDetail || {}), message:String(error.message || error) },
          });
        }
        throw error;
      } finally { InputLockManager.unlock(lock); }
  }

  const TurnEngine = (() => {
    const hasLiveReplacement = (g,team) => !!g?.teams?.[team]?.bench?.some(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
    const currentOrderEntryCanResolve = g => {
      const [team,slot] = g?.order?.[g?.turn] || [];
      if (team == null || slot == null) return false;
      const unit = typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
      return !!(unit && !unit.dead) || hasLiveReplacement(g,team);
    };
    const normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries = g => {
      const skipped = [];
      const orderLength = Math.max(0,Number(g?.order?.length || 0));
      if (!orderLength || g?.winner || g?.ended) return skipped;
      for (let inspected = 0; inspected < orderLength && !currentOrderEntryCanResolve(g); inspected++) {
        const [team,slot] = g.order[g.turn] || [];
        const unit = team != null && slot != null && typeof unitAt === "function" ? unitAt(g,team,slot) : null;
        skipped.push(Object.freeze({teamId:team ?? null,slot:slot ?? null,entityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0)}));
        BattleEventEngine.emit(g,"EMPTY_SLOT_TURN_SKIPPED",{
          teamId:team ?? null,
          sourceEntityId:unit?.entityId || unit?.uid || null,
          payload:{slot:slot ?? null,turnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial||0),reason:"DEAD_OR_EMPTY_WITHOUT_LIVE_REPLACEMENT"},
        });
        g.turn = (Number(g.turn || 0) + 1) % orderLength;
        g.turnSerial = Number(g.turnSerial || 0) + 1;
        g.preparedKey = null;
        g.dualActorController = null;
        if (g.turn === 0) g.round = Math.max(1,Number(g.round||1)) + 1;
        if (typeof checkWinner === "function") checkWinner(g);
        if (g.winner || g.ended) break;
      }
      return skipped;
    };

    return Object.freeze({
      end:(...args)=>endTurn(...args),
      current:g=>g?.order?.[g.turn] || null,
      phase:g=>g?.phase || "IDLE",
      normalizeDeadEmptyOrderEntries,
      currentOrderEntryCanResolve,
    });
  })();

            usedSkill: !!usedSkill,
            switched: !!switched,
            requestedAt: performance.now(),
            turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          };
          return;
        }
        if (
          cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") &&
          !switched &&
          dualActorController.current(g, u) === "beast" &&
          !u.dead &&
          unitAt(g, t, s)?.uid === u.uid
        ) {
          u.dualUsedSkill = !!(u.dualUsedSkill || usedSkill);
          dualActorController.toRider(g, u);
          log(g, `${u.name}: 빙마 행동 종료 → 기수 행동 시작.`, "turn");
          return;
        }
        let used = !!(usedSkill || u.dualUsedSkill);
        u.dualUsedSkill = false;
        endTurn(g, t, s, used);
      }
      function executeCoreBase(g, t, s, a, { sim = false } = {}) {
        if (a?.type === "deploy") {
          const current = g.order?.[g.turn];
          if (!current || current[0] !== t || current[1] !== s || typeof a.unit !== "string") return false;
          const deployed = deployCharacter(g, t, s, a.unit);
          if (!deployed) return false;
          endTurn(g, t, s, false);
          return true;
        }
        const preUnit = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (preUnit && !preUnit.dead && preUnit.speedDelayTurns > 0 && a?.type !== "speedDelay") return false;
        if (a?.type === "speedDelay") {
          if (!preUnit || preUnit.dead || preUnit.speedDelayTurns <= 0) return false;
          preUnit.speedDelayTurns--;
          preUnit.lastAction = "speedDelay";
          log(g, `${preUnit.name}: Speed ${card(preUnit).speed} 출전 지연으로 행동을 넘김.`, "turn");
          endTurn(g, t, s, false);
          return true;
        }
        if (a?.type === "switch") {
          if (!preUnit || preUnit.dead || !canSwitch(g, t, preUnit) || !g.teams[t].bench[a.bench]) return false;
          const cost = switchCost(preUnit), actor = cardHasCapability(preUnit, "dualActor") ? "beast" : null, plan = a.switchPaymentPlan;
          const currentPlan = plan ? Math.abs(plan.R + plan.W + plan.B - cost) < 0.001 && reqAcceptsCounts(parseCost("*".repeat(cost)), plan) && canPayReq(preUnit, { R:plan.R, W:plan.W, B:plan.B, any:0, wb:0 }, actor) : null;
          if (plan && !currentPlan) return false;
          const paid = plan ? spendPaymentPlan(preUnit, plan, actor) : spendFrom(preUnit, "*".repeat(cost), [], actor);
          g.teams[t].switchUses = (g.teams[t].switchUses || 0) + 1;
          log(g, `${preUnit.name}: 교체 코스트 ${cost ? `${cost}E` : "무료"} 지불, 진영 교체 ${g.teams[t].switchUses}/3.`);
          a = { ...a, switchPaid: paid };
        }
        prepareCurrentTurn(g);
        let u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (!a) return false;
        if (!u || u.dead) return false;
        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence") {
          const scheduledExecution = u.scheduledAction
            ? beginScheduledActionExecution(g, u)
            : null;
          if (scheduledExecution?.duplicate) {
            endTurn(g, t, s, true);
            return true;
          }
          const executed = executeScheduled(g, t, s, u, sim);
          if (!executed) return false;
          if (scheduledExecution) completeScheduledActionExecution(g, scheduledExecution);
          endTurn(g, t, s, true);
          return true;
        }
        let usedSkill = false,
          switched = false;
        if (a.type === "dualPass") {
          u.lastAction = "pass";
          log(
            g,
            `${u.name}: ${a.actor === "rider" ? "기수" : "빙마"} 행동을 넘김.`,
          );
        } else if (a.type === "gather") {
          let actor = a.actor || null,
            store = energyStore(u, actor),
            amt = status(u, "slow") ? 0.5 : 1,
            colors = naturalColors(g, t, u, amt, actor, "natural-gather"),
            existingHalf = amt === 0.5 ? store.find((e) => e.a === 0.5) : null,
            col = a.gatherProposal
              ? consumeNaturalGatherProposal(g, t, s, u, amt, actor, a.gatherProposal, "natural-gather")
              : existingHalf
                ? existingHalf.c
                : a.color && colors.includes(a.color)
                  ? a.color
                  : g.rng.pick(colors),
            max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
          if (!col) return false;
          if (existingHalf)
            log(
              g,
              `${u.name}: 기존 ${col} 반에너지와 합치기 위해 자연 수급 색이 ${col}로 고정.`,
            );
          if (totalEnergy(u, actor) + amt > max) {
            let ri = a.replaceIndices ?? a.replaceIndex;
            if (ri == null && (t !== "P" || g.mode === "aivai"))
              ri = bestEnergyReplacementPlan(u, col, amt, actor);
            if (ri != null) {
              let indices = Array.isArray(ri) ? ri : [ri],
                old = indices
                  .map((i) => store[i])
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .map((e) => `${e.c}${e.a === 0.5 ? "½" : ""}`)
                  .join("+");
              let gained = gainEnergy(g, u, col, amt, indices, actor);
              if (!gained) {
                log(g, `${u.name}: 선택한 에너지 양이 부족해 새 ${col} 에너지를 포기.`);
              } else
              log(
                g,
                `${u.name}${actor ? ` ${actor === "beast" ? "빙마" : "기수"}` : ""}: 최대 에너지에서 ${old} 버림 → ${col}${amt === 0.5 ? "½" : ""} 보관.`,
              );
            } else log(g, `${u.name}: ${col} 에너지 포기(최대치).`);
          } else {
            gainEnergy(g, u, col, amt, null, actor);
            log(
              g,
              `${u.name}${actor ? ` ${actor === "beast" ? "빙마" : "기수"}` : ""}: ${col} 에너지 ${amt} 획득.`,
            );
          }
          u.lastAction = "gather";
        } else if (a.type === "switch") {
          switchCharacter(g, t, s, a.bench);
          u.lastAction = "switch";
          switched = true;
        } else if (a.type === "skill") {
          let baseSk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
            sk = baseSk,
            cost = a.costOverride != null
              ? String(a.costOverride)
              : effectiveSkillCost(g, t, u, baseSk, baseSk?.cost),
            actor = a.actor || null;
          if (!baseSk || u.skillSeals?.[baseSk.id] || !skillRequirementsMet(u, baseSk)) return false;
          if (a.copySkill) {
            let source = CARDS.find((c) => c.id === a.copyCard);
            sk = source?.skills.find((x) => x.id === a.copySkill);
            if (!sk) return false;
          }
          let plan = a.paymentPlan;
          if (plan) {
            let current = findPaymentPlan(
              g,
              t,
              u,
              skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : cost,
              actor,
              (c) =>
                Math.abs(c.R - plan.R) < 0.001 &&
                Math.abs(c.W - plan.W) < 0.001 &&
                Math.abs(c.B - plan.B) < 0.001,
            );
            if (!current) return false;
          } else if (!canPayAction(g, t, u, cost, actor)) return false;
          let noTarget = ["field"].includes(baseSk.target),
            target =
              baseSk.target === "enemySlot" || noTarget
                ? null
                : unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot);
          if (
            !noTarget &&
            baseSk.target !== "enemySlot" &&
            (!target || target.dead)
          )
            return false;
          if (skillDefHasRuntimeKind(baseSk, "openingGuard")) {
            if (u.firstGuardUsesRemaining <= 0) return false;
            u.firstGuardUsesRemaining = 0;
            log(g, `${u.name}: 퍼스트가드 사용 횟수 소모(0/1).`);
          }
          let paid = plan
            ? spendPaymentPlan(u, plan, actor)
            : spendAction(g, t, u, cost, a.prefer, actor);
          if (skillDefHasEffect(baseSk, "hurricaneDive") && a.extraW) {
            let extra = spendReq(
              u,
              { R: 0, W: a.extraW, B: 0, any: 0, wb: 0 },
              [],
              actor,
            );
            paid.push(...extra);
          }
          log(
            g,
            `${u.name}: ${a.copySkill ? `드래곤메모리로 ${sk.name}` : baseSk.name} 사용 (실제 코스트 ${paid.map((x) => x.c + (x.a === 0.5 ? "½" : "")).join("") || "없음"}).`,
          );
          const effectiveActionTargets = (() => {
            const requested = (a.targetUids || []).map(uid => findUnit(g, uid)).filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
            if (requested.length) return requested;
            if (effectiveSkillTarget(u, baseSk) === "enemyField") return g.teams[other(t)].field.filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
            return [target].filter(unit => unit && !unit.dead);
          })();
          const effectiveActionSpec = a.effectiveActionSpec || PlusStarModifierOwner.actionSpec(g, t, s

      function openTimeAccelerationGather(a,t,s,u) {

      function playerGather(a) {
        const [t, s] = game.order[game.turn],
          u = unitAt(game, t, s),
          actor = a.actor || null;
        if (u && timeAccelerationIsActive(game,u)) return openTimeAccelerationGather(a,t,s,u);
        if (u && (cardHasCapability(u,"jungleMud") || cardHasCapability(u,"alternatingCharge"))) return openCustomNaturalGather(a,t,s,u);
        const store = energyStore(u, actor),
          amt = status(u, "slow") ? 0.5 : 1,
          existingHalf = amt === 0.5 ? store.find((e) => e.a === 0.5) : null,
          proposal = previewNaturalGatherProposal(game, t, s, u, amt, actor, "natural-gather"),
          col = proposal?.color,
          max = card(u).maxEnergy || 4;
        if (!proposal || !col) return;
        a.gatherProposal = proposal;
        if (existingHalf) log(game, `${u.name}: 기존 ${col} 반에너지와 합치기 위해 자연 수급 색이 ${col}로 고정.`);
        if (totalEnergy(u, actor) + amt <= max + .001) {
          const executed = execute(game, t, s, a);
          if (executed) afterAction();
          return;
        }
        const overflow = totalEnergy(u, actor) + amt - max,
          root = $("#modalContent"), seen = new Set(), items = [];
        store.forEach((e, i) => {
          if (Number(e.a || 0) + .001 < overflow) return;
          const key = `${e.c}:${e.a}`;
          if (seen.has(key)) return;
          seen.add(key);
          items.push({i, e});
        });
        root.innerHTML = `<h2>에너지 최대</h2><p>새 ${col}${amt === 0.5 ? "½" : ""}을 보관하려면 기존 에너지 <b>하나만</b> 버리세요.${Math.abs(totalEnergy(u, actor)-3.5)<.001 ? " 현재 3.5E이므로 반에너지 하나도 버릴 수 있습니다." : ""}</p><div class="choice-list energy-single-discard"></div><div class="choice-list"><button id="discardNew">새 에너지 버리기</button><button id="gatherCancel">취소</button></div>`;
        const list = root.querySelector('.energy-single-discard');
        for (const {i,e} of items) {
          const b = document.createElement('button');
          b.textContent = `${e.c}${e.a === .5 ? '½' : ''} 하나 버리고 새 ${col}${amt === .5 ? '½' : ''} 보관`;
          b.onclick = () => {
            $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
            a.replaceIndex = i;
            delete a.replaceIndices;
            delete a.discardNew;
            if (execute(game, t, s, a)) afterAction();
          };
          list.appendChild(b);
        }
        if (!items.length) list.innerHTML = `<p class="muted">에너지 하나만 버려서는 새 에너지를 보관할 수 없습니다.</p>`;
        root.querySelector("#discardNew").onclick = () => {
          $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
          a.discardNew = true;
          delete a.replaceIndex; delete a.replaceIndices;
          if (execute(game, t, s, a)) afterAction();
        };
        root.querySelector("#gatherCancel").onclick = () => $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        $("#modal").classList.add("open");
      }
